# BOXING CENTER COLOMIERS — MASTER BLUEPRINT

> **COLOMIERS IS WHERE THE QUESTION STARTS. BOXING CENTER IS WHERE THE ROUTE RESOLVES.**

This file is the consolidated implementation authority for `boxingcenter-colomiers.fr`. It preserves the detailed 16-part blueprint developed for the project while removing the inter-part conversational handoffs from the original raw record. It is intended to be handed directly to the Astro implementation agent.

## 0.1 — How to use this document

- Treat verified real-world truth and the claim registry as higher authority than any prose example.
- Treat later locked doctrine as authoritative when an earlier section records exploration that was subsequently refined.
- Do not invent missing business facts to satisfy a layout. Missing or conflicted facts remain missing/conflicted until verified.
- Do not redesign the site from scratch. Implement the system defined here.
- Implementation convenience never outranks geographic truth, accessibility, canonical ownership, or the locked design language.

## 0.2 — Canonical terminology reconciliation

- **THE CORNER** = the service posture: the site behaves like a knowledgeable corner that helps the visitor understand what they are seeing and what to do next.
- **THE RESOLVING ROUTE** = the final signature visual/interaction mechanism that expresses uncertainty resolving into a real Boxing Center destination.
- **PROXIMITY AS PRODUCT / THE PROXIMITY ENGINE** = the product mechanism that converts origin + intent + verified programme facts into transparent orientation.
- **PRECISION UNDER PRESSURE** = the overarching art-direction/interaction quality.
- **RED IS EARNED** = red is a semantic signal of supported resolution, not decorative saturation.
- **BOXING CENTER — DEPUIS COLOMIERS** is the preferred public contextual identity where wording could otherwise imply a physical Colomiers branch. The domain/project may still be called Boxing Center Colomiers.

## 0.4 — Global immutable decisions

- No physical Boxing Center is represented as existing in Colomiers; Colomiers is origin/context, never a fake branch.
- The site must provide standalone decision value and must not operate as an SEO doorway.
- Minimes and Portet are real physical destinations only where current verified data supports them.
- The homepage owns the broad local intent; every other indexable route owns a distinct human/search problem.
- The Proximity Engine is deterministic, transparent and claim-backed; there is no opaque AI score or fake percentage match.
- No fake route time, distance precision, address, map pin, local reviews or Google Business Profile may be manufactured.
- The Resolving Route is the signature interface mechanism; route geometry must encode meaning rather than decorate empty space.
- Red is earned by supported resolution; it does not saturate the interface by default.
- More certainty means less motion. Native scrolling is preserved. Motion never owns critical meaning.
- Mobile transforms the composition rather than receiving a reduced or impoverished version.
- Real photography carries physical intensity; documentary imagery must never be fabricated or misattributed.
- The main editorial voice uses respectful tutoiement; facts precede adjectives; certainty never exceeds evidence.
- No LocalBusiness schema is created for Colomiers. Visible HTML is canonical truth; JSON-LD confirms it.
- One fact is authored once and projected into HTML, machine documents, metadata, comparisons and recommendation logic.
- No React, GSAP, Lenis, Three.js, CMS, database, public MCP or LLM runtime API is required at launch.
- Astro is static-first; JavaScript/hydration is earned only by genuine product-state requirements.
- WCAG 2.2 AA is the release floor. The decision remains usable without pointer, animation or JavaScript where technically reasonable.
- Performance budgets are enforced; advanced visuals cannot block comprehension or the primary decision.
- Analytics measures decisions, not identities. No PII enters analytics; session replay/fingerprinting are excluded.
- QA evidence is part of the definition of done. A visually impressive staging build is not sufficient proof of completion.

## 0.5 — Definitive pre-launch factual blockers

1. **Opening hours:** The formal brief and more recent public destination material conflict (21h15 vs 21h30). Verify authoritative hours before publication.
2. **Minimes address:** Previous public material contained conflicting street-number references. Reverify the canonical physical address before structured-data publication.
3. **Discipline matrix:** Reconfirm current discipline availability per physical club, including boxe anglaise, MMA, grappling/JJB, kick-boxing, fitness/Lady, Baby Boxe and boxe éducative.
4. **Schedules:** Obtain an authoritative current production schedule source. If no reliable synchronization exists, publish only a manually verified snapshot with freshness.
5. **Pricing/offers:** Confirm offer validity, network-wide vs location-specific scope, child prices, duration and payment conditions.
6. **Child age bands:** Confirm exact age/programme bands per club; never infer one club's bands across the network.
7. **First-session policies:** Verify equipment lending, clothing, arrival, contact/sparring expectations and trial procedure per relevant club.
8. **Contact:** Reconfirm the brief-supplied phone `05 62 24 46 82` and recipient `bc.combat31@gmail.com` immediately before launch.
9. **Media provenance:** Complete the asset manifest: real location, subject, rights, crop, alt/caption; obtain adequate Portet imagery if Portet is visually represented.
10. **Organization/branch relationship:** Confirm the real organizational relationship before emitting structured properties such as `branchOf`.

Business launch decisions still required: production host/adapter; GPTBot training policy; final analytics stack/consent implementation; whether location reviews are displayed.

## 0.6 — Implementation sequence

1. Foundation: Astro, tokens, layout, route/metadata registry, header/footer.
2. Truth system: entities, claims, media provenance, club components, schema/machine outputs.
3. Homepage static core: hero, truth statement, disciplines, real destinations.
4. Decision product: Intent Selector, Proximity Engine, transparent Recommendation Result.
5. Inner discipline routes and page-specific content systems.
6. Practical systems: planning, pricing, first session and contact.
7. Motion enhancement only after static hierarchy passes review.
8. Analytics/consent only after the functional product exists.
9. Hardening: accessibility, performance, SEO/GEO, privacy, security, full QA and production smoke.

# PART 01 — Foundation, Truth & Positioning


## 01.0 — The correction that changes the entire project

The original brief describes the site as a local SEO acquisition property for Colomiers, whose purpose is to capture people searching for boxing/MMA around Colomiers and redirect them toward Boxing Center Minimes and Portet.

That business objective stays.

**The implementation philosophy cannot stay exactly as written.**

Google's current spam policy explicitly identifies as doorway abuse things such as:

- domains or pages targeting specific cities and funneling users elsewhere;
- pages generated mainly to send visitors to the genuinely useful destination;
- multiple geographically targeted properties that are substantially similar. (Google for

Developers)

Therefore:

**boxingcenter-colomiers.fr cannot be an SEO doorway.**

It must become an actual **Colomiers decision product**.

The user should receive meaningful value **before ever clicking Minimes or Portet**.

That distinction is non-negotiable.


## 01.1 — What this website IS


### Canonical definition

**Boxing Center Colomiers is a local sports-combat discovery and orientation platform for** **people living in or around Colomiers who want to practise boxing, MMA or related** **combat sports through the Boxing Center network.**

It does **not** represent a Boxing Center gym physically located in Colomiers.

Its job is to answer:

**“I live in Colomiers and I want to box / practise MMA / get fit / find something for my child. What is actually available to me, and which Boxing Center should I choose?”**

That is much stronger than:

“Club de boxe Colomiers — click here to visit Portet.”


## 01.2 — What this website is NOT

This gets locked into the implementation contract.

It is **not**:

- a sixth Boxing Center location;
- a fake local gym;
- a Google Business Profile for Colomiers;
- a virtual office;
- a generic landing page;
- an affiliate-like doorway;
- a duplicate of Minimes;
- a duplicate of Portet;
- a copy of boxe-toulouse.com with “Colomiers” inserted;
- a pile of city keywords;
- a promotional microsite;
- an online checkout;

- a fake timetable for a non-existent Colomiers gym;
- a fake address;
- a fake map pin;
- a fake local review profile;
- a page pretending that Minimes or Portet is “in Colomiers.”

The brief itself correctly prohibits implying that a physical Boxing Center is located in Colomiers.

Google's Business Profile guidance reinforces the underlying principle: real-world business information must reflect an actual location accurately; virtual addresses and non-existent storefronts are not valid substitutes for real physical presence. (Google Support)


## 01.3 — The positioning transformation


### A — Original brief

Capture “Boxe Colomiers,” “MMA Colomiers,” etc. and redirect visitors to existing clubs.


### B — Our version

**Own the question “Where should someone from Colomiers practise combat sports?” better than any actual gym website answers it.**

This is materially different.

We're not pretending proximity.

We're **solving proximity**.


## 01.4 — Core strategic proposition

I want one sentence underneath almost every future decision:

**COLOMIERS IS THE STARTING POINT. BOXING CENTER IS THE DESTINATION. THE SITE EXPLAINS THE JOURNEY.**

That is the conceptual spine.

It allows search relevance without geographical deception.


## 01.5 — The first genuine +1

This is not yet the final art-direction +1.

It is the **product +1**.


### THE PROXIMITY ENGINE

Most local SEO pages answer:

“Where are we?”

This site answers:

**“Given where YOU are and what YOU want to do, where should YOU go?”**

The site progressively turns:

**location + intent + discipline + profile + schedule preference**

into:

**recommended real Boxing Center**


### Example

Visitor searches:

MMA Colomiers

We should not simply display:

MMA à Colomiers Découvrez Boxing Center!

Instead:

**YOU'RE LOOKING FOR MMA FROM COLOMIERS.**

Then:

**MMA available:** Portet **Why:** cage + MMA programme + grappling + related combat disciplines **Alternative:** whatever other verified Boxing Center genuinely supports that need **Next:** view actual MMA programme / first-session guidance / route.

The result provides a useful answer **inside Colomiers' site**.

That is the difference between **funnel** and **decision tool**.


## 01.6 — The experience metaphor

This site should not behave like another boxing-club brochure.

Our base metaphor:


### THE CORNER

In boxing, your corner does not fight for you.

It tells you:

- where you are;
- what you're seeing;
- what to do next;
- what fits your situation;
- where your opening is.

boxingcenter-colomiers.fr becomes the **digital corner for a Colomiers prospect**.


### User arrives confused:

Boxe or MMA? Which club? Is it far? Can beginners go? What does my child do?

Is there a women's class? How much? Which timetable works? Do I need gear?

The site progressively removes each uncertainty.

At the end there should be essentially **one remaining action**:

Go to the real club.


## 01.7 — Primary user groups

We are not designing for a vague “sports enthusiast.”

We need distinct jobs.


### Persona A — The absolute beginner

Likely query:

boxe colomiers club boxe colomiers cours boxe débutant colomiers

Mental state:

“I want to try this but I don't know whether I belong there.”

Needs:

- reassurance;
- beginner-compatible discipline;
- what happens on first arrival;
- equipment;
- expected intensity;
- no-sparring reassurance;
- recommended location;
- schedule;
- next step.


### Persona B — The MMA seeker

Likely query:

MMA Colomiers club MMA Colomiers

Mental state:

“Where can I actually train MMA near here?”

Needs:

- real MMA availability;
- cage / grappling information if verified;
- coach/programme information;
- actual venue;
- actual timetable;
- travel decision;
- related disciplines.

This user should probably reach **Portet faster** than someone looking purely for traditional English boxing.

That's orientation.

Not funneling.


### Persona C — The parent

Likely query:

boxe enfant Colomiers cours boxe enfant Colomiers

Mental state:

“Is boxing appropriate and safe for my child?”

Needs:

- ages;
- educational-boxing explanation;
- physical contact explanation;
- supervision;

- values;
- timetable;
- equipment;
- exact physical club;
- beginner arrival procedure.

The original brief explicitly asks for reassurance around discipline, confidence, coordination and respect.


### Persona D — Fitness / female beginner

Likely query:

boxing fitness colomiers boxe femme colomiers

Mental state:

“I want the training benefits without feeling like I'm joining Fight Club.”

Needs:

- non-competitive entry;
- fitness benefits;
- women-specific formats where actually available;
- no expectation of competition;
- atmosphere;
- recommendation based on programme rather than gender stereotypes.

The brief explicitly wants this area framed around cardio, fitness, accessibility and reassurance.


### Persona E — Experienced boxer

Likely query:

club boxe anglaise colomiers boxe anglaise colomiers

Mental state:

“Skip the marketing. Show me ring, coach, timetable and level.”

Needs:

- serious training availability;
- equipment;
- competitive possibilities;
- coach credentials;
- timetable;
- real location.

We must not make the entire website beginner-soft at the cost of credibility.


### Persona F — Search engine / answer engine

Google, Bing, ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, Perplexity, etc.

Needs:

- one unambiguous entity model;
- explicit relationship between Colomiers and real clubs;
- stable facts;
- crawlable HTML;
- canonical URLs;
- structured routes;
- page-level intent;
- explicit source relationships;
- no geographical hallucination;
- concise answerable passages;
- consistent organisation data.

This persona is treated as seriously as a visual visitor.


## 01.8 — The three layers of truth

The future site gets a hierarchy of factual authority.


### Layer 1 — Network truth

Examples:

- Boxing Center identity;
- number of genuine clubs;

- network-level services;
- canonical network URLs.

One source.


### Layer 2 — Location truth

Minimes and Portet each own:

- their address;
- opening hours;
- disciplines;
- coaches;
- equipment;
- schedule;
- location-specific pricing if applicable;
- directions;
- reviews if shown.

**Colomiers is not allowed to invent or independently maintain these.**


### Layer 3 — Colomiers orientation truth

Colomiers owns:

- which search intent exists;
- what the user is trying to solve;
- how real clubs compare for that need;
- proximity/orientation information;
- decision guidance;
- local Colomiers context.

This is where our original value exists.


## 01.9 — CRITICAL: no duplicated factual databases

This is one of the strongest lessons from your existing ecosystem.

Right now the network often stores essentially the same facts in:

HTML `llms.txt`

`llms-full.txt` assistant context schema hardcoded components SEO copy

That creates drift.

Our new principle:

**FACT ONCE → RENDER EVERYWHERE.**

For Astro I already want something structurally close to:

```text
src/
data/
network.ts
locations/
minimes.ts
portet.ts
disciplines.ts
colomiers-intents.ts
```

Then:

HTML JSON-LD metadata comparison interface LLM documents FAQs assistant data

**are generated from that authority wherever possible.**

No six separately typed copies.


## 01.10 — Claim states

Every fact should eventually support:

```text
type ClaimState =
| "verified"
| "time-sensitive"
| "pending"
| "conflicted"
| "prohibited"
```

Example:

{ claim: "Portet opening hours", value: "10:00–21:30", state: "verified", source: "...", verifiedAt: "2026-09-07" }

This sounds excessive.

It is not.

It's how we stop machines from publishing confident lies.


## 01.11 — We already have a factual conflict

The specification says both Minimes and Portet should be presented as open:

**6 days / week, 10:00–21:15**

But the current public material we examined for your existing sites exposes **21:30** for Minimes and Portet.

Therefore:


### Status

**CONFLICTED.**

We do **not** silently choose 21:15.

We do **not** silently choose 21:30.

In the eventual registry:

CLAIM-HOURS-001 Subject: Boxing Center Minimes Brief: 10:00–21:15 Current Minimes site: 10:00–21:30 Status: CONFLICTED Action: verify authoritative operating hours before publication

Same for Portet if needed.

That's what Second Brain means in practice.


## 01.12 — Entity graph

The site should have a mental entity graph even before we write JSON-LD.

```text
Boxing Center
│
├── Boxing Center Minimes
│ ├── physical location: Toulouse
│ ├── disciplines
│ ├── coaches
│ ├── schedule
│ └── offers
│
├── Boxing Center Portet
│ ├── physical location: Portet-sur-Garonne
│ ├── disciplines
│ ├── coaches
│ ├── schedule
│ └── offers
│
└── boxingcenter-colomiers.fr
├── audience area: Colomiers
├── local information
├── discipline discovery
├── comparison/orientation
└── referrals to verified physical locations
```

Note the deliberate absence of:

```text
Boxing Center Colomiers
└── physical location: Colomiers
```

It doesn't exist.


## 01.13 — Structured-data principle

**We should not mark the Colomiers site itself up as a fake LocalBusiness in Colomiers.**

Google's `LocalBusiness` documentation identifies the business's physical address as a required property for that supported structured-data feature. (Google for Developers)

Google also recommends using the most specific organization subtype that genuinely matches the entity. (Google for Developers)

Therefore our likely graph is closer to:


### Root site

WebSite


Organization

representing the genuine Boxing Center organisation/network where appropriate.


### Real venue references

Proper real-world entities for:

- Minimes;
- Portet.

With their **actual** location information.


### Pages

`WebPage` / appropriate semantic objects connected back to the organisation.

Not:

{ "@type": "HealthClub", "name": "Boxing Center Colomiers", "address": "Colomiers" }

Absolutely forbidden unless a genuine location opens there later.


## 01.14 — Naming system

This matters tremendously.


### Header identity

Potentially:

**BOXING CENTER DEPUIS COLOMIERS**

rather than:

**BOXING CENTER COLOMIERS**

when wording could imply a physical facility.

We can still use `boxingcenter-colomiers.fr` as the domain.

But the interface can distinguish:


### Brand

BOXING CENTER


### Context

COLOMIERS

This tiny difference helps entity truth.


## 01.15 — Public language matrix


### Allowed

- Boxe près de Colomiers
- Où faire de la boxe depuis Colomiers
- Cours de boxe accessibles depuis Colomiers
- MMA proche de Colomiers
- Clubs Boxing Center les plus proches de Colomiers
- Vous habitez Colomiers ?
- Trouvez le club adapté depuis Colomiers
- Pratiquer la boxe quand on habite à Colomiers
- Boxing Center accueille les pratiquants venant de Colomiers
- Depuis Colomiers, comparez Minimes et Portet


### Contextually allowed for SEO

The literal phrase:

“boxe Colomiers”

can naturally occur when discussing the **search/intention/topic**.

Example:

Vous cherchez où pratiquer la boxe à Colomiers ou à proximité ?

Fine.


### Forbidden

- Notre salle de Colomiers
- Boxing Center situé à Colomiers
- Venez dans notre club à Colomiers
- Votre club Boxing Center de Colomiers
- Nos coachs de Colomiers

- Nos cours à Colomiers
- Horaires Boxing Center Colomiers
- Adresse Boxing Center Colomiers
- Ring de Colomiers
- Salle MMA Colomiers

unless someday factually true.


## 01.16 — Local SEO philosophy

We are **not** going to write:

club boxe Colomiers, boxe anglaise Colomiers, MMA Colomiers, sport de combat Colomiers...

in hidden blocks or absurd repetitive prose.

Google specifically calls out unnatural repetition and blocks of geographic terms as keyword stuffing. (Google for Developers)

Instead:


### Location establishes context.


### Intent establishes route.


### Content establishes relevance.


### Evidence establishes authority.

That's our SEO grammar.


## 01.17 — What gives this domain standalone value?

This is the key anti-doorway answer.

The Colomiers site must contain original capabilities the destination sites do not collectively provide.


### Candidate permanent value modules

**1. Club selector**

“Which one fits me?”

**2. Discipline decision system**

Not just discipline descriptions.

Something like:

I WANT TO… → learn striking → try MMA → get fit → train without competition → find something for my child → train seriously

Then expose appropriate real disciplines.

**3. Colomiers-to-club orientation**

Real geographic comparison.

Not marketing language.

**4. Schedule comparison**

Not duplicate schedules.

An orientation layer answering:

“Which actual club has a session compatible with what I need?”

**5. First-session navigator**

User profile → what happens → where to go.

**6. Real-club comparison**

Minimes vs Portet based on verified differences.

**7. Local FAQ**

Questions someone specifically living in Colomiers would genuinely ask.

If we execute those properly, the site is not a doorway anymore.

It's an **interface on top of the network**.


## 01.18 — Conversion model

We need several layers rather than a giant “JOIN NOW” button.


### Conversion 0 — understanding

Visitor understands there is no Colomiers location.

Success.


### Conversion 1 — self-identification

Visitor selects:

discipline / goal / age / experience.


### Conversion 2 — location recommendation

Visitor understands which real venue is appropriate.


### Conversion 3 — intent action

Depending on context:

- view real timetable;
- see real location;
- call;
- contact;
- first-session pathway;
- inspect pricing.


### Conversion 4 — commercial conversion

Occurs on appropriate Boxing Center property.

This separation prevents the Colomiers domain from becoming a fake ecommerce shell.


## 01.19 — CTA vocabulary

We should stop using one universal CTA.


### Discovery

**TROUVER MON CLUB**


### Discipline

**OÙ PRATIQUER LE MMA ?**


### Comparison

**COMPARER MINIMES & PORTET**


### Timetable

**VOIR OÙ JE PEUX M'ENTRAÎNER**


### Beginner

**PRÉPARER MA PREMIÈRE SÉANCE**


### Destination

**DÉCOUVRIR MINIMES**

**DÉCOUVRIR PORTET**

The wording itself communicates truth.


## 01.20 — Contact form rethink

The PDF requests:

- prénom;
- nom;
- téléphone;
- email;
- activité;
- message;
- club or zone.

Fine as a requirement.

But B is:


### First screen

QU'EST-CE QUE TU CHERCHES ?

Options:

- Boxe anglaise
- MMA
- Fitness
- Enfant
- Je ne sais pas encore

Then optionally:

QUEL CLUB ?

- Recommandez-moi

- Portet

Then minimal contact.

This turns the form itself into an orientation device.

But it still remains fast.


## 01.21 — Primary site success metric

Not:

“How many pages ranked?”

Not:

“How long users stayed?”

Our core product KPI:


### QUALIFIED DESTINATION RATE

Percentage of visitors who, after consuming meaningful Colomiers guidance, take a qualified next action toward a real Boxing Center location.

Examples:

- destination click;
- timetable click;
- first-session action;
- phone action;
- qualified contact.

Supporting metrics later:

organic impressions non-brand queries

SERP CTR decision-tool usage club recommendation completion route-level engagement form completion outbound destination conversion AI referral traffic brand search lift.


## 01.22 — Visual implication of the foundation

Even before choosing the design system, the foundation gives us a visual rule:

**The website should feel like movement from ambiguity toward certainty.**

Therefore the scroll should not merely alternate:

image text cards image CTA.

It should progressively **resolve**.

Possible conceptual progression:

UNKNOWN ↓ WHERE ARE YOU STARTING? ↓ WHAT ARE YOU LOOKING FOR? ↓ WHAT FITS YOU? ↓ WHICH CLUB? ↓ WHAT WILL HAPPEN? ↓ GO.

That gives the page narrative direction.


## 01.23 — Mobile implication

On mobile this becomes even stronger.

Not “desktop minus effects.”

A person searches:

MMA Colomiers

on their phone.

Within the first meaningful viewport sequence they should understand:

**YES — BOXING CENTER IS ACCESSIBLE FROM COLOMIERS.**

**NO — THERE ISN'T A FAKE COLOMIERS LOCATION.**

**YES — I CAN FIND THE RIGHT REAL CLUB HERE.**

Then one thumb can move through the decision.


## 01.24 — Accessibility implication

The orientation engine **cannot depend on animation**.

Every recommendation must remain:

- keyboard operable;
- logically ordered;
- available without pointer-hover;
- comprehensible without motion;
- understandable to screen readers;
- available with JavaScript failure where technically reasonable.

Animation may communicate movement.

It must not own the information.


## 01.25 — Performance implication

No giant WebGL hero simply because the reference bank contains WebGL.

Our foundation asks:

Does this mechanism improve the idea of orientation/proximity?

If not:

**remove it.**

That remains consistent with the motion doctrine: motion should guide attention, communicate state or preserve continuity; otherwise it should not exist. (Google for Developers)

Baffled Bar does **not** mean expensive pixels.

It means intentionality that feels impossible.


## 01.26 — Foundation acceptance gate

Before Part 02 is allowed to override this, every later decision must pass these questions:


### Truth

Does it accidentally imply a physical Colomiers club?

If yes → reject.


### Value

Would this page still help someone if all outbound links disappeared?

If no → doorway risk → redesign.


### Originality

Is this just Minimes/Portet content rewritten with “Colomiers”?

If yes → reject.


### Search intent

Does the page solve a distinct user problem?

If no → merge/delete.


### GEO

Can an AI correctly explain the relationship between Colomiers, Boxing Center, Minimes and Portet after reading only this page?

If no → fail.


### UX

Does the user become more certain after this section?

If no → fail.


### Accessibility

Can the same decision be reached without seeing animation or using a mouse?

If no → fail.


### Performance

Is complexity justified by communication value?

If no → remove.


### Baffled

Could this entire mechanism be replaced by a normal local SEO template without meaningfully changing the experience?

If yes →

**we are nowhere near done.**


## 01.27 — LOCKED FOUNDATION

So our current foundation is:

**Boxingcenter-colomiers.fr is not a virtual Boxing Center location and not an** **SEO doorway. It is a standalone local orientation experience that helps people** **starting from Colomiers understand combat sports, identify their needs,** **compare appropriate real Boxing Center locations, and confidently choose** **their next physical step.**

And the first project-level innovation is:


### PROXIMITY AS PRODUCT

Instead of hiding the absence of a Colomiers gym, we transform that truth into the site's primary useful mechanism.


<a id="part-02-search-territory-keyword-architecture-canonical-ownership"></a>

# PART 02 — Search Territory, Keyword Architecture & Canonical Ownership

Part 01 established the rule:

**We do not manufacture a Boxing Center in Colomiers. We solve the problem of finding the right Boxing Center from Colomiers.**

Part 02 now determines **what this website is allowed to rank for, which page owns each** **search intent, what pages we refuse to create, and how humans + Google + answer** **engines understand the architecture.**

This part is foundational enough that I would lock it before writing a single final page.


## 02.0 — First: the live search battlefield is harder than the brief suggests

The PDF gives us priority terms such as:

- Club de boxe Colomiers
- Boxe Colomiers
- Club MMA Colomiers
- MMA Colomiers
- Sport de combat Colomiers
- Cours de boxe Colomiers
- Boxe anglaise Colomiers
- Boxing fitness Colomiers
- Boxe enfant Colomiers
- “près de Colomiers” variants.

But live search research changes how we should attack them.

There are **real physical competitors inside Colomiers**.

USC Boxing is an actual Colomiers association with a physical address, planning, pricing and disciplines. Its current site is very simple, but its local entity truth is strong. (uscboxing.fr)

Maison du Fight explicitly presents MMA, pieds-poings and grappling **in Colomiers**, with children/adults and a physical venue. (Maison du Fight)

CrossFit Colomiers has a dedicated boxing page targeting “Club de Boxe à Colomiers,” with boxe anglaise, kick-boxing, cardio boxing, a physical address, trial CTA and timetable/pricing paths. (crossfitcolomiers.com)

École d'Arts Martiaux de Colomiers also publishes MMA, grappling, lutte and martial-arts content from a real Colomiers address. (Ecole d'Arts Martiaux de Colomiers)

Even just outside Colomiers, CFC — Cornebarrieu Fitness Center explicitly positions itself as two minutes from Colomiers and offers a large sports-combat ecosystem. (CFC31.fr)


### Strategic consequence

We cannot defeat these sites by simply writing:

“Club de boxe Colomiers” 19 times.

And because we do not physically operate there, we **should not try to imitate their** **local-business signals**.

Their advantage:

**physical presence.**

Our advantage must become:

**information superiority + decision superiority + network depth + UX superiority + factual reliability.**


## 02.1 — What we are actually trying to own

Our search territory is not merely:

BOXE + COLOMIERS.

It is:

**SPORTS DE COMBAT × COLOMIERS × INTENT × PROFILE × DECISION**

That multiplication is where the defensible search surface appears.

For example:

**Weak commodity page**

MMA Colomiers Le MMA est un sport qui mélange plusieurs disciplines...

Every MMA website can produce that.

**Our page**

Vous habitez Colomiers et cherchez du MMA ? Voici où Boxing Center propose réellement du MMA, ce que vous trouverez sur place, quel niveau est nécessaire, ce qui se passe lors d'une première séance, et si Portet ou une autre option répond réellement à votre besoin.

That cannot be written convincingly without knowing the network.

That is **non-commodity content**.

Google's current 2026 guidance for generative AI Search explicitly prioritizes unique, non-commodity, useful content and warns against manufacturing pages for every query variation. (Google for Developers)

That fits our strategy perfectly.


## 02.2 — Search architecture principle

The PDF recommends many possible URLs, including:

```text
/club-boxe-colomiers
/boxe-anglaise-colomiers
/club-mma-colomiers
/boxing-fitness-colomiers
/boxe-enfant-colomiers
/sport-combat-colomiers
```

**We do not implement that list literally.**

Otherwise we create:

/ ↓ /club-boxe-colomiers

↓ /sport-combat-colomiers ↓ /boxe-anglaise-colomiers

with three pages competing for essentially the same broad intent.

That's classic cannibalization architecture.


## 02.3 — THE CANONICAL OWNER RULE

Every meaningful search cluster gets:

**ONE PRIMARY PAGE.**

Every supporting page may mention the subject.

Only one page actively owns it.

Formula:

QUERY CLUSTER ↓ SEARCH INTENT ↓ CANONICAL OWNER ↓ SUPPORTING PAGES ↓ CONVERSION DESTINATION

No ambiguity.

This is the first major architectural change I recommend locking.

That reads like SEO machinery.

The content, titles and H1s establish local relevance.

The URLs remain clean.


## 02.5 — Pages I explicitly reject


### ❌ /club-boxe-colomiers/

Homepage owns this intent.


### ❌ /sport-combat-colomiers/

Homepage owns the broad sports-de-combat cluster.


### ❌ /sports-de-combat-colomiers/

Same query family.


### ❌ /club-mma-colomiers/

`/mma/` owns it.


### ❌ /boxe-femme-colomiers/

At launch, not enough evidence that this deserves an independent page separate from fitness/female beginner intent.

It belongs inside:

```text
/boxing-fitness/
```

If Search Console later proves a distinct substantial search behaviour and we have enough **genuinely distinct content**, we reconsider.

Not before.

## 02.7 — Priority tiers

Important:

**These are strategic priorities, not claimed keyword-volume estimates.**

We have not run an authoritative keyword-volume database, so we do not invent search volumes.


### P0 — BUSINESS-CRITICAL

Broad acquisition:

**boxe Colomiers club de boxe Colomiers MMA Colomiers club MMA Colomiers sport de combat Colomiers**

These establish domain relevance.


### P1 — DISCIPLINE / COMMERCIAL

**boxe anglaise Colomiers cours de boxe Colomiers boxing fitness Colomiers boxe enfant Colomiers**

Closer to a concrete need.


### P2 — DECISION / REASSURANCE

These may be extremely valuable even if lower-volume:

**boxe débutant Colomiers où faire du MMA près de Colomiers boxe femme Colomiers première séance boxe Colomiers club boxe près Colomiers**

These are where our content can be substantially better than generic competitors.


### P3 — PRACTICAL INTENT

**planning horaires tarifs prix matériel âge enfants**

Often closer to conversion.


## 02.8 — Homepage ownership

Homepage becomes the **broad intent authority**.

It owns:

boxe Colomiers club de boxe Colomiers sports de combat Colomiers sports de combat près de Colomiers

But its public language remains truthful.


### Proposed <title>

**Boxe, MMA & sports de combat près de Colomiers | Boxing Center**

Notice:

Not:

Club Boxing Center Colomiers


### Proposed H1


### Où pratiquer la boxe ou le MMA quand on habite à Colomiers ?

Excellent for:

human clarity search relevance entity truth answer engines.


### First semantic answer

Very early in visible HTML:

Boxing Center ne possède pas de salle physique à Colomiers. Les pratiquants venant de Colomiers sont orientés vers les clubs du réseau les plus adaptés, notamment Toulouse Minimes et Portet-sur-Garonne, selon la discipline recherchée, le niveau et les disponibilités.

That's an **answer-engine-grade statement**.

No ambiguity.


## 02.9 — /boxe-anglaise/


### Owns

**boxe anglaise Colomiers cours de boxe Colomiers secondary: club boxe anglaise proche Colomiers**


### Title

**Boxe anglaise près de Colomiers | Cours Boxing Center**


### H1


### Faire de la boxe anglaise depuis Colomiers

Not:

Boxe anglaise Colomiers — meilleur club boxe Colomiers.


### Page must answer

What English boxing actually is.

Can complete beginners join?

Which real Boxing Center offers it?

What differs between the relevant locations?

What does a session contain?

Which classes suit beginners?

What equipment?

What about competitive training?

Which timetable?

What next?


## 02.10 — /mma/

This is strategically difficult because **real MMA exists physically in Colomiers**.

Therefore we must not publish generic:

“MMA Colomiers — Boxing Center.”

We need a useful angle.


### Title

**MMA près de Colomiers | Où s’entraîner avec Boxing Center**


### H1


### Tu cherches du MMA depuis Colomiers ? Voilà où t’entraîner.


### Content ownership

The page answers:

MMA vs grappling vs striking.

What is available in the relevant Boxing Center.

Which physical facility has the appropriate equipment.

Beginner pathway.

How the first MMA session works.

Cross-training possibilities.

Real timetable.

Real destination.


### Key principle

Do not compete with local physical MMA operators by pretending we're one.

Compete by having the **best answer to “is Boxing Center a good MMA option for someone** **living in Colomiers?”**

Much more defensible.


## 02.11 — /boxing-fitness/

The brief asks us to combine fitness and female audiences.

I agree **for launch**.

But the page must not imply:

women = fitness only.

So information architecture becomes:


### Boxer pour la forme. Sans obligation de combattre.

Then pathways:

fitness-focused female-only programme where verified mixed sessions boxing camp / physical preparation beginner access.

Gender is a programme/access dimension.

Not a stereotype.


## 02.12 — /boxe-enfants/

This could be extremely strong.


### Cluster

boxe enfant Colomiers cours boxe enfant Colomiers boxe éducative Colomiers baby boxe Colomiers boxe ado Colomiers


### Title

**Boxe enfant près de Colomiers | Baby Boxe & boxe éducative**


### H1


### Quelle boxe pour un enfant qui habite à Colomiers ?

That question is significantly better than keyword copy.


### Parent intent hierarchy

A parent does not initially care about SEO terms.

They care about:

**AGE ↓ SAFETY ↓ CONTACT LEVEL ↓ COACHING ↓ SCHEDULE ↓ LOCATION ↓ PRICE**

The entire page should follow that exact mental sequence.


## 02.13 — New page: /premiere-seance/

This does **not** exist explicitly in the client deliverables.

I am adding it.

Why?

Because your own portfolio proved repeatedly that **first-session uncertainty is one of the** **richest conversion territories in this industry.**

Portet, Minimes and Saint-Cyprien already contain strong versions of this mechanism.

This site can turn it into a network orientation experience.


### Search ownership

boxe débutant Colomiers premier cours boxe Colomiers essayer boxe Colomiers je n'ai jamais fait de boxe faut-il acheter des gants est-ce qu'on doit faire du sparring


### Proposed H1


### Jamais boxé ? Voilà exactement ce qui va se passer.

That is copy written for fear.

Not Google.

Google gets the semantic content naturally.


## 02.14 — New page: /quel-club/

This may become the website's **signature utility**.

It has some SEO value.

Its primary purpose is product differentiation.


### H1


### Minimes ou Portet : lequel te correspond depuis Colomiers ?

The page should eventually compare only verified attributes:

discipline available facilities schedule suitability children's classes MMA availability English-boxing depth relevant audience transport/travel factors if verified.

It must never manufacture superiority.


## 02.15 — /plannings/ must change conceptually

The brief asks for a Planning page linking directly to Minimes.

A lazy implementation would be:

PLANNING Cliquez ici pour voir Minimes.

That is practically the doorway-page definition.

Rejected.


### Baffled version


### Quand peux-tu t'entraîner depuis Colomiers ?

Then:

**What discipline?**

**What day?**

**What time window?**

Then show verified options in the actual relevant clubs.

The source data stays owned by each real location.

Colomiers becomes the **comparison/orientation interface**.

Then:

Voir le planning complet de Minimes

with a descriptive crawlable link.

Google specifically recommends descriptive anchor text because it helps both people and Google understand destination pages. (Google for Developers)


## 02.16 — /tarifs/ must also become useful

Again, not:

Tarifs → click Minimes.

Instead:


### Combien coûte la boxe quand on habite à Colomiers ?

Then explain verified network options.

For example:

season standard subscription children first session multi-location access

only after source verification.

And then clearly:

Finaliser l'inscription sur la boutique Boxing Center.

Pricing facts should come from our future claim registry.

Not manually rewritten.


## 02.17 — Contact is not an SEO landing page

```text
/contact/
```

No keyword stuffing.

Its job:

**resolve a person.**

Possible H1:


### Tu hésites encore ? Dis-nous ce que tu cherches.

Then our orientation form.

Search relevance comes from site structure.

We do not need:

Contact club boxe Colomiers MMA sport combat Toulouse...


## 02.18 — Search intent architecture

There are fundamentally **six mental states**.

DISCOVER "I want to do combat sports." ↓ CHOOSE "Boxing or MMA?" ↓ VALIDATE "Can someone like me do it?" ↓ LOCATE "Where do I actually go?" ↓

PLAN "When and how much?" ↓ ACT "Let me start."

Pages should correspond to those states.

That means the website architecture models **human progression**, not just keyword categories.


## 02.19 — Search journey examples


### Search:

**MMA Colomiers**

Entry:

```text
/mma/
```

Journey:

WHAT MMA IS ↓ BEGINNER? ↓ WHAT BOXING CENTER ACTUALLY OFFERS ↓ WHICH REAL LOCATION ↓ SCHEDULE ↓ FIRST SESSION


### Search:

**boxe enfant Colomiers**

Entry:

```text
/boxe-enfants/
```

Journey:

AGE ↓ TYPE OF PRACTICE ↓ SAFETY ↓ WHAT CHILD LEARNS ↓ WHERE ↓ WHEN ↓ PRICE ↓ CONTACT / DESTINATION


### Search:

**club boxe Colomiers**

Entry:

/

Journey:

NO FAKE LOCATION ↓ WHAT DO YOU WANT? ↓ DISCIPLINES ↓ MINIMES / PORTET ↓ DECISION ENGINE

Search architecture cannot be page islands.

The graph becomes intentional.

```text
HOME

│

│

└── PORTET EXTERNAL
```

Then:

`/tarifs/` and /plannings/

link back to discipline pages contextually.

Not just navigation-menu links.


## 02.21 — Anchor-text system

Bad:

En savoir plus

Repeated everywhere.

Bad for information scent.

Better:

Voir les cours de boxe anglaise

Comparer Minimes et Portet

Voir les créneaux MMA

Comprendre une première séance

Consulter le planning officiel de Minimes

Découvrir Boxing Center Portet

Google recommends descriptive, concise anchor text that makes sense even when read largely out of context. (Google for Developers)

Perfect UX principle as well.


## 02.22 — No city-page factory

This becomes an explicit prohibition.

We will **not** launch:

/boxe-blagnac/ /boxe-tournefeuille/ /boxe-pibrac/ /boxe-cornebarrieu/ /boxe-beauzelle/ /mma-blagnac/ /mma-tournefeuille/

simply because they are nearby.

That would move us straight back toward geographic doorway architecture.

Google's spam policy specifically identifies city/region-targeted pages or domains that funnel visitors elsewhere as doorway abuse. (Google for Developers)

Neighbouring communes may appear naturally where relevant.

No factory.


## 02.23 — Keyword placement rules

The PDF correctly says keywords must be integrated naturally rather than excessively.

Our precise contract:


### Primary query concept should usually appear naturally in:

`<title>` H1 or equivalent main title concept opening copy at least one supporting heading if natural relevant internal-link anchor image alt only when image genuinely depicts it.

Not:

every heading every card every alt every paragraph.

Google's Search Essentials says to use the words people search for in prominent places such as the page title and main heading, while its title guidance explicitly warns against repeated and stuffed titles. (Google for Developers)


## 02.24 — No SEO word-count target

The brief requests “textes longs et structurés.”

We reinterpret that as:

**complete coverage, not arbitrary length.**

Google explicitly states there is no preferred word count and now reiterates that there is no ideal page length for AI Search either. (Google for Developers)

Therefore:

MMA page may require 1,700 words.

A contact page may require 250.

A decision interface may communicate equivalent information through tables and structured data.

Length follows **decision complexity**.


## 02.25 — Content density rule

Every meaningful page gets three layers.


### Layer A — FAST ANSWER

For user arriving from Google.

First 5–15 seconds.


### Layer B — DECISION CONTENT

Comparisons, options, practical answers.


### Layer C — DEPTH

Evidence, detailed explanations, related questions.

This gives us the unusual combination we want:

**feels short** while **being deep**.


## 02.26 — GEO / AI answer architecture

Important correction from some of your existing work:

`llms.txt` can still be useful for systems that choose to consume it.

But it is **not a Google GEO ranking trick**.

Google's 2026 guidance explicitly says no AI-specific text file or special markup is required for AI Overviews/AI Mode, and that Google Search ignores `llms.txt` for ranking/search visibility. (Google for Developers)

Therefore:


### We may still generate:

```text
/llms.txt
/llms-full.txt
```

because your broader agent ecosystem can benefit.

But:

**The visible HTML remains canonical.**

And machine files derive from the same data source.

Never the reverse.


## 02.27 — GEO answer surfaces

Each major page should contain several **extractable factual responses**.

Example MMA:


### Can I practise MMA at Boxing Center if I live in Colomiers?

Yes. Boxing Center does not operate a physical gym in Colomiers, but residents can access MMA through the appropriate nearby network location. The Colomiers site helps identify the relevant club and current timetable.

Then evidence/links.

Example children:


### Is there Boxing Center children's boxing in Colomiers?

Not:

Yes.

Correct:

Boxing Center does not currently operate a children's class in a physical Colomiers location. Families from Colomiers can access the network's children's programmes at the relevant nearby Boxing Center clubs.

This difference is **massive** for machine truth.


## 02.28 — No fake FAQ rich-result strategy

We'll absolutely have excellent questions and answers.

**But we will not sell ourselves the fantasy that adding FAQPage schema magically dominates Google.**

Google significantly restricted FAQ rich results to authoritative government and health websites. (Google for Developers)

Therefore FAQ is built for:

users information architecture long-tail relevance answer extraction assistants

—not to game a SERP decoration.


## 02.29 — SERVER-RENDERED SEARCH CONTENT

Because we're using Astro, we have an advantage.

All primary search content should ship as actual HTML.

No critical information exclusively inside:

canvas WebGL post-hydration JS CSS generated `content` hover states.

Google recommends keeping meaningful textual content available in the DOM; semantic HTML also improves access for other users and tools. (Google for Developers)

So later, when our design becomes insane:

**THE VISUAL EXPERIENCE CAN BE WILD.**

The semantic foundation remains boringly excellent.

That is Baffled Bar.


## 02.30 — FILTER / QUERY PARAMETER RULE

Suppose:

```text
/quel-club/?discipline=mma
```

or:

```text
/plannings/?jour=mercredi&discipline=boxe
```

These are useful interface states.

They are **not automatically search pages**.

Default:

canonical → clean parent URL

No indexable combinatorial explosion.

We do not create:

/plannings/mma-mercredi-colomiers/ /plannings/mma-jeudi-colomiers/ /plannings/boxe-mardi-colomiers/

Absolutely not.
