These are provisional copy—we polish during page-level architecture.

But the **structure is locked**.


## 02.32 — H1 must NOT simply clone title

Example:


### <title>

**MMA près de Colomiers | Où s'entraîner avec Boxing Center**


### H1

**Tu cherches du MMA depuis Colomiers ? Voilà où t'entraîner.**

Search understands it.

Human feels spoken to.

Design has character.


## 02.33 — Meta-description system

Descriptions do not become keyword dumps.

They answer:

**What? for whom? why this page? what next?**

Example homepage:

Vous habitez Colomiers et cherchez de la boxe, du MMA ou un sport de combat ? Comparez les disciplines et trouvez le Boxing Center adapté entre Minimes et Portet.

Example MMA:

MMA, grappling, niveau débutant, équipements et créneaux : découvrez quelle salle Boxing Center choisir lorsque vous venez de Colomiers.


## 02.34 — Search-content provenance

Every page gets internal provenance.

Not necessarily displayed like Wikipedia.

But data architecture tracks:

CLAIM SOURCE SOURCE LOCATION LAST VERIFIED OWNER TIME SENSITIVITY

Especially for:

prices times coaches disciplines addresses age bands equipment.

This allows us later to render things such as:

Planning vérifié le 7 septembre 2026.

Trust signal for human.

Freshness signal operationally.


## 02.35 — Competitive-content strategy

We cannot beat genuine Colomiers venues at:

**being physically in Colomiers.**

So don't fight the impossible battle.

We beat weak competing pages at:


### Depth

Their pages often provide only basic discipline/location information.


### Decision support

Most make users figure out what suits them.

We do the reasoning.


### Beginner UX

Your own previous sites already demonstrate strong first-session storytelling.

We can make this site the best of those mechanisms.


### Cross-club intelligence

No single Colomiers competitor can explain Boxing Center's network as well as Boxing Center itself.


### Truth

We explicitly show where the training actually happens.

Paradoxically, being transparent about the weakness creates trust.


## 02.36 — Search snippet strategy

A searcher asks:

**MMA Colomiers**

Competing result:

Maison du Fight – Club de MMA, Boxe pieds-poings...

Strong because it's local.

Our result:

**MMA près de Colomiers | Où s'entraîner avec Boxing Center** Vous habitez Colomiers ? Comparez le MMA, le grappling, le niveau débutant et les créneaux du Boxing Center adapté.

Different job.

Google has a reason to show both.

That's what we want.

Not a counterfeit version of theirs.


## 02.37 — Content should answer before selling

Page sequence:

ANSWER ↓ PROVE ↓ ORIENT ↓ REASSURE

↓ ACT

Not:

BUY ↓ BUY ↓ 29€ ↓ BUY ↓ BUY

This matches the client requirement to avoid aggressive promotional mechanics.

And is stronger search content.


## 02.38 — Search architecture performance contract

Every SEO-critical page should be:

server-rendered crawlable fast usable without hydration internally linked self-canonical included in sitemap free of accidental `noindex`.

Interactive layers progressively enhance it.


## 02.39 — Search Console launch architecture

From day one, we should monitor performance by **intent cluster**, not merely page.

Example internal reporting:

BROAD LOCAL boxe colomiers club boxe colomiers sport combat colomiers MMA mma colomiers club mma colomiers mma près colomiers KIDS boxe enfant colomiers baby boxe colomiers boxe éducative colomiers

Then track:

impressions position distribution CTR landing page destination clicks qualified actions.

Google's 2026 guidance also now exposes generative-AI performance through Search Console, so we should include that visibility in post-launch monitoring rather than relying on invented “GEO scores.” (Google for Developers)


## 02.40 — Cannibalization test

For every future page ask:

**What query would this page deserve to rank for that another page doesn't already deserve more?**

If answer is unclear:

**do not publish the page.**


## 02.41 — Doorway test

For every future page:

If we remove all external Boxing Center links, does this page still give a Colomiers resident substantial value?

If:

**NO**

→ page fails.


## 02.42 — Commodity-content test

Ask:

Could a generic AI write 90% of this without knowing Boxing Center?

If yes:

**fail.**

Google itself now emphasizes non-commodity content for AI-powered Search. (Google for Developers)

Our pages should require real network knowledge.


## 02.43 — Search architecture acceptance gate

Before any route becomes indexable:


### 1. Unique intent

One canonical query territory.


### 2. Genuine standalone usefulness

Useful before outbound click.


### 3. Geographic truth

Never invents Colomiers presence.


### 4. Distinct from sibling routes

No cannibalization.


### 5. Original network evidence

Contains something only Boxing Center can meaningfully explain.


### 6. Human-first

Doesn't read like SEO copy.


### 7. Crawlable

Critical information in HTML.


### 8. Linkable

Has useful inbound/outbound/internal relationship.


### 9. Answerable

AI can extract a truthful answer.


### 10. Convertible

There is an obvious next human step.


## 02.44 — LOCKED SEARCH ARCHITECTURE

My recommendation is therefore to **reject the keyword-per-page interpretation of the brief** and lock this smaller, stronger ecosystem:

```text
/
├── boxe-anglaise/
├── mma/
├── boxing-fitness/
├── boxe-enfants/
├── premiere-seance/
├── quel-club/
├── plannings/
├── tarifs/
└── contact/
```

With:


### Homepage

owns broad Colomiers combat-sports intent.


### Discipline pages

own distinct disciplinary intent.


### First Session

owns beginner fear/reassurance.


### Quel Club

owns proximity and decision.


### Planning + Pricing

provide **actual orientation value**, rather than acting as backlink shells.


### Contact

resolves unresolved people.

And this gives us our second major project doctrine:


### **DON'T BUILD PAGES FOR KEYWORDS.

BUILD AUTHORITIES FOR INTENTS.**

Google sees fewer, materially better pages.

AI sees clean entities and direct answers.

Users see a website built around what they're actually trying to decide.

And our designers now have something significantly more interesting than:

hero → disciplines → cards → SEO paragraph → footer.


<a id="part-03-information-architecture-user-journey"></a>

# PART 03 — Information Architecture & User Journey


## 03.0 — OBJECTIVE OF THIS PART

Part 01 established **what the site is**.

Part 02 established **what search territory each page owns**.

Part 03 establishes:

**HOW A HUMAN MOVES THROUGH THE SYSTEM.**

This includes:

- global navigation;
- homepage narrative;
- search-entry journeys;
- club-selection logic;
- discipline-selection logic;
- cross-page continuity;
- conversion hierarchy;
- external handoff;
- mobile navigation;
- footer architecture;
- browser history behavior;
- loading states;
- empty states;
- errors;
- 404;
- no-JavaScript behavior;
- accessibility of the entire journey.

The site must never feel like a collection of pages.

It should feel like **one continuous decision**.


## 03.1 — THE EXPERIENCE PRINCIPLE

The visitor begins with uncertainty.

The site ends with certainty.

Therefore the experience follows:

UNKNOWN ↓ ORIENT ↓ IDENTIFY ↓ COMPARE ↓ REASSURE ↓ PLAN ↓ GO

Every major section must remove uncertainty.

If a section merely decorates the page without helping the visitor:

**remove it.**


## 03.2 — THE CENTRAL UX QUESTION

At all times, the website should know what question it is answering.

There are only seven major questions:

1. WHAT IS AVAILABLE? 2. IS IT FOR ME? 3. WHERE DO I ACTUALLY GO? 4. WHICH CLUB FITS ME? 5. WHEN CAN I TRAIN? 6. WHAT DOES IT COST? 7. HOW DO I START?

Everything else is supporting evidence.

This is extremely important.

The navigation should not expose the internal structure of Boxing Center.

It exposes the **questions users have**.


## 03.3 — INFORMATION ARCHITECTURE: FINAL PRIMARY TREE

```text
BOXINGCENTER-COLOMIERS.FR
/
│
├── /boxe-anglaise/
│
├── /mma/
│
├── /boxing-fitness/
│
├── /boxe-enfants/
│
├── /premiere-seance/
│
├── /quel-club/
│
├── /plannings/
│
├── /tarifs/
│
├── /contact/
│
├── /mentions-legales/
│
└── /confidentialite/
```

But the user does **not** experience those routes as ten equal menu entries.

That would be poor information architecture.

We group them around intent.


## 03.4 — GLOBAL NAVIGATION


### Desktop primary navigation

Recommended structure:

[BOXING CENTER / COLOMIERS] DISCIPLINES TROUVER MON CLUB PLANNINGS TARIFS [COMMENCER]

That is it.

Not nine links.

Not a mega-menu containing every SEO route.


### Why?

The four navigation jobs are:


### DISCIPLINES

“What can I do?”


### TROUVER MON CLUB

“Where should I go?”


### PLANNINGS

“When?”


### TARIFS

“How much?”


### COMMENCER

“How do I start?”

That's the visitor's mental model.


## 03.5 — “DISCIPLINES” MENU

Desktop may expose a lightweight panel:

DISCIPLINES 01 BOXE ANGLAISE Technique · loisir · compétition 02 MMA Debout · sol · grappling 03 BOXING FITNESS Cardio · remise en forme · sans obligation de combat 04 ENFANTS Baby Boxe · éducative · adolescents

No giant mega-menu.

No cards-within-cards.

No unnecessary imagery.

Navigation is not a homepage.


## 03.6 — THE PRIMARY CTA

Instead of:

S'INSCRIRE

our global persistent CTA becomes:


### COMMENCER

This is deliberate.

At Colomiers-domain level, “register” is premature.

We still need to determine:

- discipline;
- profile;
- destination.

“Commencer” represents the complete pathway.

Selecting it opens or navigates to the first-session/orientation flow.


## 03.7 — HEADER STATE SYSTEM

The header should not remain visually static through the entire journey.

But it also should not constantly mutate.

I recommend **three states**.


### STATE A — HERO

Minimal.

Brand + menu + Commencer.

Potentially transparent or visually fused with hero composition.


### STATE B — CONTENT

After hero threshold:

compact persistent header.

Clear background/material.

Navigation always accessible.


### STATE C — DECISION MODE

When inside `/quel-club/`, selector, planning or another tool:

header becomes quieter.

The current task dominates.

Example:

BOXING CENTER TROUVER MON CLUB [FERMER]

The user knows they have entered a focused decision state.


## 03.8 — DO NOT HIDE NAVIGATION ON SCROLL DOWN

A common “premium site” trick is:

scroll down → header disappears scroll up → header appears.

We should be cautious.

This site is decision-heavy.

The user repeatedly needs:

- club;
- planning;
- pricing;
- beginning.

Navigation availability has more value than cinematic minimalism.

Recommended:

**compact sticky header throughout**, with only subtle density changes.


## 03.9 — CURRENT PAGE SIGNAL

Current route is visibly and programmatically marked.

Example:

```text
DISCIPLINES
└─ MMA ← current
```

Use semantic `aria-current="page"` where appropriate.

W3C explicitly recommends identifying the current page and keeping menu terminology/order consistent across responsive variants.


## 03.10 — DESKTOP ≠ HAMBURGER BY DEFAULT

Desktop should expose primary choices.

Hiding everything behind a hamburger because it looks “luxury” is not sophistication.

It adds interaction cost.

Desktop gets direct navigation.

Mobile gets an intentional full-screen navigation experience.


## 03.11 — HOMEPAGE JOB

The homepage does not have to explain everything.

Its job is:

**TURN A COLOMIERS SEARCHER INTO AN ORIENTED PERSON.**

The homepage should progressively answer:

WHY AM I HERE? ↓ WHAT CAN I DO? ↓ WHICH DIRECTION FITS ME? ↓ WHERE WILL I ACTUALLY TRAIN? ↓ WHAT SHOULD I DO NEXT?


## 03.12 — HOMEPAGE MASTER SCROLL

This is the current recommended architecture.

00 NAVIGATION 01 HERO — THE QUESTION 02 TRUTH STRIP — THE GEOGRAPHY 03 INTENT SELECTOR — WHAT ARE YOU LOOKING FOR? 04 DISCIPLINE WORLD 05 PROXIMITY ENGINE 06 TWO REAL DESTINATIONS 07 BEGINNER / FIRST SESSION 08 PLANNING WINDOW 09 TRUST / EVIDENCE 10 FINAL ORIENTATION 11 FOOTER

Now we design each structurally.


## 03.13 — SECTION 01: HERO

Not:

BIENVENUE CHEZ BOXING CENTER COLOMIERS

Forbidden both creatively and factually.

Instead:


### **TU PARS DE COLOMIERS.

LE BON CLUB DÉPEND DE CE QUE TU CHERCHES.**

Supporting line:

Boxe anglaise, MMA, fitness, enfants : trouve la discipline et le Boxing Center qui correspondent réellement à ton besoin.

Primary action:

**TROUVER MON CLUB**

Secondary:

**VOIR LES DISCIPLINES**


## 03.14 — HERO VISUAL IDEA

We do not yet lock exact art direction—that comes later.

But structurally, the hero should communicate:

**ORIGIN → POSSIBILITY → DESTINATION.**

It should not simply display:

fighter + gradient + title.

Potential visual logic:

```text
COLOMIERS
│
├──────── BOXE ─────── MINIMES
│
├──────── MMA ──────── PORTET
│
├──────── FITNESS ───── ...
│
└──────── ENFANTS ───── ...
```

Not literally as Google Maps.

More like a **living route system**.

That can become the foundation of our visual language later.


## 03.15 — HERO MOTION

The motion's job:

**establish relationship.**

Not “wow.”

Example sequence:

1. COLOMIERS exists alone. 2. A route line starts extending. 3. disciplines appear as possible directions. 4. actual destinations begin resolving. 5. headline resolves. 6. CTA becomes active.

Duration should remain controlled.

No 12-second intro.

No loading theatre.

User can act immediately.


## 03.16 — SECTION 02: TRUTH STRIP

Immediately after hero:

**PAS DE SALLE BOXING CENTER PHYSIQUE À COLOMIERS. ET C'EST JUSTEMENT POUR ÇA QUE CETTE PAGE EXISTE.**

Then:

Elle t'aide à trouver le bon club du réseau depuis Colomiers, selon la discipline, ton niveau et tes disponibilités.

This is an unusually strong piece of positioning.

Instead of hiding the perceived weakness:

**we lead with it.**


## 03.17 — WHY SO EARLY?

Because geographical ambiguity later feels deceptive.

Geographical clarity immediately feels confident.

It also gives answer engines an unmistakable factual statement.

The client's brief explicitly forbids presenting a Boxing Center as physically located in Colomiers.


## 03.18 — SECTION 03: INTENT SELECTOR

This may become one of our signature components.

Headline:


### QU'EST-CE QUE TU VEUX TROUVER ?

Not:

Nos disciplines.

Options:

JE VEUX... APPRENDRE À BOXER ESSAYER LE MMA ME REMETTRE EN FORME TROUVER UN COURS POUR MON ENFANT M'ENTRAÎNER SÉRIEUSEMENT JE NE SAIS PAS ENCORE

The system asks what the visitor **wants**, rather than expecting them to know product terminology.


## 03.19 — SELECTOR INTERACTION

Click:

**ME REMETTRE EN FORME**

The interface does not instantly send them somewhere.

It transforms.

Example:

TU CHERCHES SURTOUT... ○ CARDIO ○ TECHNIQUE ○ COURS ENTRE FEMMES ○ UN PEU DE TOUT

Then:

→ BOXING FITNESS → BOXING CAMP → LADY PROGRAMME (if verified)

Then:

**VOIR OÙ PRATIQUER**

This is not a quiz for entertainment.

It is **progressive decision compression**.


## 03.20 — URL STATE

Selector choices should be shareable where useful.

Possible:

```text
/quel-club/?objectif=mma
```

or History API state.

But these parameter combinations are UI states, not automatically indexable SEO pages.

This follows Part 02.


## 03.21 — NO-JAVASCRIPT FALLBACK

Without JS:

the selector becomes normal HTML links:

Apprendre à boxer → /boxe-anglaise/ Essayer le MMA → /mma/ Me remettre en forme → /boxing-fitness/ Cours pour mon enfant

→ /boxe-enfants/

The product still works.

Enhancement adds intelligence.

It does not create basic usability.


## 03.22 — SECTION 04: DISCIPLINE WORLD

Only now do we reveal the traditional disciplines.

But not in a generic four-card grid.

Structure should communicate **difference**.

Boxe anglaise should feel distinct from MMA.

Kids should not feel like a smaller adult card.

Fitness should not feel like “other.”

Each discipline has:

NAME WHAT IT FEELS LIKE WHO IT FITS LEVEL NEEDED WHERE IT EXISTS NEXT ACTION

Maximum useful information.

Minimum card sludge.


## 03.23 — DISCIPLINE CONTENT RHYTHM

Example:


### BOXE ANGLAISE

Apprendre à bouger, défendre, frapper juste. Pour débuter, progresser ou aller vers la compétition.

**Débutant → compétition**

Then:

**OÙ LA PRATIQUER DEPUIS COLOMIERS →**


### MMA

Pieds, poings, lutte, sol. Une discipline complète, enseignée progressivement.

**Débutants acceptés → niveau avancé**

**TROUVER LE CLUB MMA →**

Each discipline answers a different concern.


## 03.24 — SECTION 05: THE PROXIMITY ENGINE

This is the first real **Baffled-Bar product moment**.

Headline:


### TU ES À COLOMIERS. OÙ VAS-TU ?

Inputs should be minimal.

Potentially:

DISCIPLINE

[ MMA ] QUAND ? [ SOIR ] PROFIL [ DÉBUTANT ]

Then system produces:

TON OPTION LA PLUS COHÉRENTE BOXING CENTER PORTET MMA Grappling Cage Créneaux compatibles Débutants acceptés [VOIR POURQUOI]

Not:

Portet is the best!

Unless objectively justified.

Use:

best match based on your selected criteria.


## 03.25 — WHY THIS IS STRONG

It combines:

- local intent;
- network knowledge;
- real programme data;
- comparison;

- personalization;
- conversion.

And it gives the Colomiers site **standalone value**.

Remove all SEO considerations and this mechanism is still useful.

That is exactly what Part 01 requires.


## 03.26 — TRANSPARENCY OF RECOMMENDATION

Every recommendation explains itself.

Example:

**Pourquoi Portet ?**

Tu as choisi MMA + débutant + soir. Portet propose actuellement des créneaux MMA et dispose de l'équipement correspondant. Le résultat est basé sur les informations de programme vérifiées pour le réseau.

This protects trust.

No black-box recommendation.


## 03.27 — NO FAKE PRECISION

We do **not** claim:

“Portet is 14 minutes away.”

unless we have a reliable routing source and known origin.

Colomiers is large.

Traffic changes.

No fabricated precision.

If later we add live mapping/routing, that's a separate verified capability.


## 03.28 — SECTION 06: THE TWO REAL DESTINATIONS

Now we finally make Minimes and Portet physical.

Headline:


### DEUX CLUBS. DEUX RAISONS D'Y ALLER.

Each destination gets a substantial identity.

Not a generic location card.


### MINIMES

Potential framing:

**LA BOXE HISTORIQUE**

Verified strengths later populated from registry.

Actual address.

Real disciplines.

Actual relevant imagery.

Actual route out.


### PORTET

Potential framing:

**LE PLATEAU MULTI-COMBAT**

Again, only if supported by verified data.

Actual address.

Real MMA/cage/equipment information.


## 03.29 — DESTINATION CARDS MUST NEVER BE SYMMETRIC FOR THE SAKE OF DESIGN

If Portet has nine meaningful attributes and Minimes six:

we do not invent three Minimes attributes to fill the grid.

Reality determines data.

Design absorbs asymmetry.


## 03.30 — EXTERNAL HANDOFF RULE

Clicking:

**DÉCOUVRIR PORTET**

goes to Portet's actual site.

Before the click, user already knows:

- why;
- where;
- what;
- what they will find.

No surprise handoff.


## 03.31 — EXTERNAL LINK VISUAL LANGUAGE

External destination links should subtly indicate that the user is leaving the Colomiers orientation layer.

Example:

**VOIR BOXING CENTER PORTET ↗**

Not required everywhere.

But especially on major handoffs.


## 03.32 — SHOULD WE USE AN INTERSTITIAL?

No.

Do not show:

“Vous allez quitter boxingcenter-colomiers.fr. Continuer?”

Unnecessary friction.

Instead, communicate destination through language and external-link cue.


## 03.33 — SECTION 07: BEGINNER MOMENT

Homepage now shifts emotionally.

After all the decision logic:


### ET SI LE VRAI PROBLÈME, C'EST JUSTE D'OSER ENTRER ?

This is where we reuse the strongest lesson from your previous Boxing Center work.

Beginner anxiety is not:

“What is boxing?”

It is:

“What happens when I walk inside?”


## 03.34 — MINI FIRST-SESSION FLOW

Homepage gets compressed preview:

01 ARRIVE Tu dis que c'est ta première fois. 02 EQUIP Le matériel prévu pour l'essai est expliqué. 03 START Échauffement avec le groupe. 04 LEARN Technique adaptée à ton niveau. 05 DECIDE Tu repars. Tu choisis ensuite.

Exact factual wording will come from verified location information.

CTA:

**VOIR MA PREMIÈRE SÉANCE**


## 03.35 — SECTION 08: PLANNING WINDOW

Not a gigantic timetable embedded into homepage.

Instead:


### QUAND EST-CE QUE TU PEUX VENIR ?

Possible quick filter:

AUJOURD'HUI DEMAIN CE SOIR CE WEEK-END

Then:

show relevant verified sessions/availability information.

Important:

If data synchronization is not robust enough for dynamic “today” claims, we **do not ship this** **live state** initially.

Fallback:

Voir les créneaux par discipline.

Truth beats novelty.


## 03.36 — PLANNING PAGE EXPERIENCE

```text
/plannings/
```

Not just table.

Three modes:


### MODE 1 — JE SAIS CE QUE JE VEUX

Filter discipline.


### MODE 2 — JE SAIS QUAND JE PEUX

Filter day/time.


### MODE 3 — JE NE SAIS PAS

Recommend likely starting options.


## 03.37 — PLANNING INFORMATION DESIGN

Desktop:

potentially comparison timeline.

Mobile:

**NOT a shrunk desktop grid.**

Mobile should use:

LUNDI ──────────────── 18:00 Boxe anglaise MINIMES Débutant accepté 19:00 MMA

PORTET Tous niveaux

Scrollable vertically.

Fast.

Readable.

Tap target comfortable.


## 03.38 — PLANNING SOURCE LABEL

Every schedule surface should expose freshness.

Example:

**Planning vérifié · 07/09/2026**

Potential:

Voir la source officielle

if useful.

This is not visual noise.

It is operational trust.


## 03.39 — SECTION 09: TRUST & EVIDENCE

No generic icon triplet:

EXPERTISE PASSION CONFIANCE

Rejected.

Evidence must be concrete.

Could include verified:

- federation affiliations;
- coaching qualifications;
- real club age/history;
- actual facilities;
- real reviews;
- real photos;
- actual schedule breadth;
- actual number of disciplines.

No invented “trusted by thousands.”


## 03.40 — REVIEWS

If Google reviews are shown:

- real location clearly identified;
- source linked;
- date/freshness where applicable;
- no combining Minimes and Portet into a fake “Colomiers rating.”

Absolutely no:

BOXING CENTER COLOMIERS ★4.7

if no such listing exists.


## 03.41 — SECTION 10: FINAL ORIENTATION

Not:


### REJOIGNEZ-NOUS DÈS MAINTENANT

Instead:


### **TU SAIS CE QUE TU CHERCHES.

IL RESTE À CHOISIR OÙ COMMENCER.**

Then two primary pathways:

TROUVER MON CLUB PRÉPARER MA PREMIÈRE SÉANCE

Contact becomes tertiary:

**J'AI ENCORE UNE QUESTION**


## 03.42 — ENTRY JOURNEYS FROM GOOGLE

Homepage is not always first.

Therefore every landing page must function independently.

```text
ENTRY: “MMA COLOMIERS”
```

User lands directly on `/mma/`.

They should not need homepage context.

Top sequence:

MMA DEPUIS COLOMIERS ↓ NO PHYSICAL COLOMIERS LOCATION — CLEAR ↓ WHAT MMA IS ↓

WHO IT FITS ↓ WHERE BOXING CENTER OFFERS IT ↓ REAL CLUB ↓ SCHEDULE ↓ FIRST SESSION


## 03.43 — ENTRY: “BOXE ENFANT COLOMIERS”

Top sequence:

AGE ↓ SAFETY ↓ TYPE OF BOXING ↓ WHERE ↓ SCHEDULE ↓ PRICE ↓ FIRST VISIT

No need to read a corporate introduction first.


## 03.44 — ENTRY: “TARIF BOXE COLOMIERS”

```text
/tarifs/
```

Top answer must immediately explain:

There is no Colomiers-specific Boxing Center tariff/location. These are the relevant verified Boxing Center options available to Colomiers residents.

Then prices.

Not 900 words before answer.


## 03.45 — THE BREADCRUMB SYSTEM

Desktop and mobile, subtle.

Example:

Accueil → MMA

Or:

Accueil → Enfants

No elaborate breadcrumbs where hierarchy is only one level deep.

But semantic BreadcrumbList may still be useful if appropriate.


## 03.46 — CONTEXTUAL BACKLINKS

The brief explicitly asks for visible redirection toward Minimes and Portet, including Minimes from Planning/Tarifs and Portet in homepage copy.

We satisfy that requirement with meaningful contexts.

Not:

cliquez ici

Instead:

**Consulter le planning officiel de Boxing Center Minimes**

Google specifically recommends descriptive, crawlable `<a href>` links and notes that good anchor text helps both people and Search understand destinations.


## 03.47 — MOBILE PHILOSOPHY

Mobile is not compressed desktop.

Mobile is likely our **primary decision environment**, because local search frequently occurs in situ/on phone.

The mobile experience should therefore become even more direct.


## 03.48 — MOBILE HERO

First screen:

COLOMIERS → BOXING CENTER TU CHERCHES QUOI ? [BOXE] [MMA] [FORME] [ENFANT] TROUVER MON CLUB →

The cinematic headline can still exist.

But utility arrives almost immediately.


## 03.49 — MOBILE NAV

Tap Menu.

Full viewport.

Not a tiny dropdown.

Structure:

BOXING CENTER COLOMIERS DISCIPLINES Boxe anglaise MMA Boxing Fitness Enfants TROUVER MON CLUB PLANNINGS TARIFS PREMIÈRE SÉANCE CONTACT ──────────────── MINIMES ↗ PORTET ↗

The real destinations are visible but separated.


## 03.50 — MOBILE NAVIGATION CONSISTENCY

W3C guidance recommends maintaining consistent wording, destination and relative order across screen sizes, even if items collapse differently.

Therefore mobile does not rename:

Trouver mon club

into:

Centres

just because of space.

Same conceptual language everywhere.


## 03.51 — MOBILE BOTTOM ACTION BAR?

Potentially yes, but only on decision-heavy pages.

Not globally by default.

Example `/mma/`:

[MMA · PORTET] [VOIR LE CLUB →]

Example `/boxe-enfants/`:

[TROUVER UN CRÉNEAU] →

Contextual.

Not:

**BUY NOW** across every screen.


## 03.52 — FOCUS ORDER CONTRACT

Visual composition may become unconventional later.

DOM order cannot become nonsense.

The logical reading sequence stays:

heading explanation choice result action

not:

action result heading decorative panel explanation

simply because CSS Grid visually rearranged it.

W3C requires focusable elements to follow an order preserving meaning and operability.

This becomes an implementation gate.


## 03.53 — KEYBOARD SELECTOR

The club/intent selector must work with:

Tab Shift+Tab Enter / Space arrow keys if implementing an actual radio group pattern.

No hover-only selection.

Selection updates should have accessible status messaging where necessary.


## 03.54 — FOCUS TRANSITIONS

When changing selector steps:

do **not** throw focus randomly to the top of page.

If a new decision panel replaces the previous one:

focus should move appropriately to the new panel heading or selected interaction context when needed.

Animation and focus should tell the **same story**.


## 03.55 — MOTION / READING ORDER RULE

Visual motion can make B appear before A visually.

Screen-reader/DOM order still needs semantic progression.

Therefore motion layer always adapts to semantic layer.

Never vice versa.


## 03.56 — PAGE-TO-PAGE TRANSITIONS

We can make these excellent later.

But they cannot:

- delay navigation;
- trap scroll;
- interfere with browser Back;
- reset focus incorrectly;
- obscure URL changes;
- require WebGL.

Conceptually:

**route becomes path.**

Example:

Colomiers route line extends → next page becomes MMA.

Potentially excellent.

But performance/motion system will determine implementation.


## 03.57 — BROWSER BACK IS SACRED

If user:

Homepage → selector → Portet recommendation → MMA

Back should behave predictably.

Do not hijack history.

If selector state uses URL/history, state restoration should be intentional.


## 03.58 — EXTERNAL HANDOFF CONTINUITY

When opening Minimes or Portet:

Default to same tab for normal navigation unless there's a strong reason otherwise.

Don't force every external link into a new tab.

Users can choose.

If new tab is deliberately used, accessible indication is required.


## 03.59 — CONTACT JOURNEY

The client's required fields include identity/contact information, activity, message and desired club/zone.

We improve the experience.

First question:


### QU'EST-CE QUE TU CHERCHES ?

Then:

Boxe anglaise MMA Fitness Enfant Je ne sais pas

Second:


### TU SAIS DÉJÀ QUEL CLUB ?

Minimes Portet Recommandez-moi

Then personal details.

This produces a **better lead** and a better visitor experience.


## 03.60 — FORM LENGTH

Do not make every field mandatory.

Likely minimum:

Prénom one contact method need/discipline

Then optional:

surname second contact method message preferred club.

Exact legal/data policy later.


## 03.61 — FORM SUCCESS

Not:

Votre message a bien été envoyé.

Only.

Better:


### ON A CE QU'IL FAUT.

Then:

Ta demande concerne MMA depuis Colomiers. L'équipe pourra t'orienter vers le club approprié.

Possible immediate useful links:

**Voir le MMA**

**Comparer les clubs**

The journey doesn't die at submission.


## 03.62 — FORM ERROR

Error messages:

- specific;
- next to field;
- programmatically associated;
- human.

Not:

ERROR 422.

Example:

**Il manque ton numéro ou ton e-mail pour qu'on puisse te répondre.**


## 03.63 — LOADING STATE

The core site should barely need visible loading.

Astro/server-rendered pages should appear as HTML immediately.

Interactive tools may calculate/filter.

Loading state should communicate:

**On compare les créneaux…**

only if something genuinely asynchronous is happening.

Never fake loading to appear sophisticated.


## 03.64 — SKELETON SCREENS

Generally avoid for simple static content.

Skeletons only if real data is delayed and maintaining layout is useful.

No decorative shimmer everywhere.


## 03.65 — EMPTY STATE

Example planning filter returns none.

Bad:

AUCUN RÉSULTAT.

Better:


### PAS CE SOIR. MAIS VOICI LE PLUS PROCHE.

Then:

next compatible session alternative discipline alternative club.

Empty state becomes assistance.


## 03.66 — SELECTOR NO-MATCH

If no club genuinely meets chosen criteria:

Do not force a recommendation.

Say:


### ON N'A PAS DE CORRESPONDANCE EXACTE.

Then:

Aucun créneau vérifié ne correspond actuellement à ces trois critères. Modifie l'horaire ou contacte l'équipe.

That is trust.


## 03.67 — OFFLINE / NETWORK FAILURE

Static site content should remain usable.

If a live data service fails:

fallback to locally cached/last verified data if responsibly designed, with freshness message.

Otherwise:

Les données en direct ne répondent pas. Consulte le planning officiel.

Never blank panel.


## 03.68 — 404 EXPERIENCE

This is a perfect place to continue our metaphor.

Not:

404 — Page Not Found.

Possible:


### **MAUVAISE DIRECTION.

PAS MAUVAIS DÉPART.**

Then:

Cette page n'existe pas. Colomiers, lui, est toujours ici.

Actions:

TROUVER MON CLUB VOIR LES DISCIPLINES RETOUR À L'ACCUEIL

Potential tiny visual:

route line reaches dead end → reroutes.


## 03.69 — 404 SEO

Real HTTP `404`.

Not soft 404 with `200`.

Noindex where appropriate.

Useful navigation remains.


## 03.70 — FOOTER IS A KNOWLEDGE MAP

Not:

logo Instagram copyright.

Footer becomes a compact final architecture.

BOXING CENTER DEPUIS COLOMIERS PRATIQUER Boxe anglaise MMA Boxing Fitness Boxe enfants

COMMENCER Première séance Trouver mon club Plannings Tarifs CLUBS RÉELS Boxing Center Minimes ↗ Boxing Center Portet ↗ AIDE Contact Téléphone INFORMATIONS Mentions légales Confidentialité

Then explicit truth:

Boxingcenter-colomiers.fr est un site d'orientation locale. Il n'existe pas de salle physique Boxing Center à Colomiers.

Beautifully set.

Not hidden.


## 03.71 — FOOTER SEARCH VALUE

Footer links are crawlable `<a href>` links.

Primary internal routes appear consistently.

But footer is not stuffed with:

boxe Colomiers MMA Colomiers sports combat Colomiers boxe enfant Colomiers...

No SEO graveyard.


## 03.72 — CROSS-PAGE “NEXT BEST ACTION”

Every page ends based on its role.


### MMA

**VOIR OÙ PRATIQUER LE MMA**


### Boxe anglaise

**CHOISIR MON CLUB**


### Children

**TROUVER LE BON CRÉNEAU**


### First Session

**JE SAIS À QUOI M'ATTENDRE → TROUVER MON CLUB**


### Planning

**PRÉPARER MA PREMIÈRE SÉANCE**

Not every page ends with same generic block.


## 03.73 — PROGRESSIVE DISCLOSURE

Complexity arrives only when user asks for it.

Homepage:

simple.

Discipline page:

more depth.

Club comparison:

high detail.

Planning:

high utility.

This keeps the site both:

**fast to understand**

and

**deep enough to trust.**


## 03.74 — NO DEAD-END CONTENT

Every informative unit must answer:

What is the sensible next question?

Example:

User learns MMA.

Next question:

Where?

Therefore link to club decision.

User learns where.

Next:

When?

Planning.

User learns when.

Next:

What happens first time?

First session.

The links follow reasoning.


## 03.75 — PAGE ENTRY / EXIT MATRIX

**Page User arrives asking Page resolves Best next step**

`/` Where can I train? available directions selector

```text
/boxe-anglai
```

`se/`

Can I box? discipline + fit club

`/mma/` Where is MMA? programme + destination

planning/club

```text
/boxing-fitn
```

`ess/`

Can I train without competing?

formats club

```text
/boxe-enfant
```

`s/`

Is it right for my child? age/safety/programme planning

```text
/premiere-se
```

`ance/`

What happens first time?

uncertainty club

`/quel-club/` Which location? recommendation real venue

`/plannings/` When? compatible time first session

`/tarifs/` How much? verified price context registration/destination

`/contact/` I still need help human routing resolution


## 03.76 — SCROLL RHYTHM

Very important for Baffled Bar.

We do not design every section at:

min-height: 100vh

Premium sites often become exhausting because every thought becomes a cinematic chapter.

Our rhythm should alternate:

IMMERSIVE ↓ DENSE ↓ INTERACTIVE ↓ QUIET ↓ IMMERSIVE ↓ UTILITY ↓ QUIET

Like boxing itself:

round rest round rest.

That is a potential domain-specific design mechanism.


## 03.77 — “ROUNDS” WITHOUT TURNING THE SITE INTO A BOXING CLICHÉ

We may use rhythm inspired by rounds.

But we do not label:

ROUND 1 ROUND 2 ROUND 3

everywhere.

That would become theme-park boxing.

Instead borrow the mechanism:

**compression / intensity / release.**

Domain recontextualization without cliché.


## 03.78 — SCROLL SHOULD CHANGE KNOWLEDGE, NOT JUST POSITION

A strong scroll sequence means:

before scroll:

I need boxing.

after three sections:

I probably need English boxing.

later:

Minimes appears more relevant.

later:

I know when I can go.

That is useful scroll progress.

Not simply:

Image moved left.


## 03.79 — THE VISUAL PROGRESS INDICATOR

Potentially subtle:

COLOMIERS ─────

- ──────── ○ ─────── ○ YOU CLUB START

As visitor progresses:

COLOMIERS ───────────────

- ─────────○ CLUB START

Not necessarily literal.

Could become an extremely refined recurring device.


## 03.80 — NO SCROLL-JACKING

Native scroll.

Potential smooth interpolation where appropriate.

But:

- trackpads feel native;
- wheel doesn't lag;
- browser Find works;
- keyboard navigation works;
- anchor links work;
- reduced motion works.

We do not imprison the user in our choreography.


## 03.81 — RESPONSIVE INFORMATION PRIORITY

Desktop can show:

comparison side-by-side.

Mobile cannot just stack two 500px cards and call it responsive.

Mobile comparison can transform:

WHAT MATTERS TO YOU? [MMA] → Portet [BOXE ANGLAISE] → compare [ENFANTS] → options

Responsive means **transforming the information model**.

Not shrinking columns.


## 03.82 — MOBILE PARITY TEST

Any desktop “wow” mechanism must answer:

What is the equally intentional mobile version?

Not:

hidden under 768px.

If the answer is “hide it”:

mechanism is not yet designed.

This comes directly from our Fusion Baffled gate: mobile parity is eliminatory.


## 03.83 — NO CAROUSEL DUMP

Avoid:

testimonials carousel discipline carousel coaches carousel facilities carousel clubs carousel

all on same page.

Carousels can solve horizontal inventory.

They should not become our default space-saving mechanism.


## 03.84 — POINTER MICROSTATES

Desktop:

Links can preview destination context.

Example hover on:

**PORTET**

could subtly expose:

MMA GRAPPLING BOXE 61 ROUTE D'ESPAGNE

But all information remains accessible without hover.

Hover is enrichment.

Never access.


## 03.85 — CLUB SELECTOR PERSISTENCE

Interesting opportunity:

Once user selects:

**MMA → PORTET**

the site may keep lightweight session context.

On `/tarifs/`:

**Pour ton choix : Portet · MMA**

On `/premiere-seance/`:

**Ta première séance à Portet**

Only if implementation remains clear and privacy-light.

No need for account.

Potential sessionStorage or URL state.


## 03.86 — USER CONTROL

Any remembered selection has:

**CHANGER**

visible.

Never trap them inside personalization.


## 03.87 — ANALYTICS EVENTS

We will eventually track decision behavior, not creepy surveillance.

Examples:

intent_selected discipline_selected club_recommended club_changed planning_filtered first_session_opened destination_clicked contact_started contact_completed

This lets us understand where the decision architecture fails.


## 03.88 — NO DARK PATTERNS

The brief already forbids marketing traps and aggressive promotional mechanics.

Therefore:

no fake scarcity no fake countdown no hidden close buttons no forced phone capture before information no default newsletter opt-in no preselected consent no “only 2 spots remaining” without authoritative real inventory.


## 03.89 — NAVIGATION ACCESSIBILITY GATE

Navigation must satisfy:

- semantic <nav>;
- sensible accessible label;
- visible focus;
- logical tab order;
- current-page indication;
- touch-capable targets;
- consistent wording;
- no hover dependency;
- escape closes modal menu;
- focus returns to menu trigger;
- background interaction blocked while modal menu is open.

W3C specifically emphasizes keyboard interoperability, meaningful menu structure and predictable navigation.


## 03.90 — INTERNAL LINK GATE

Every indexable page:

- has at least one contextual inbound internal link;
- has contextual outbound internal links;
- is reachable through normal crawlable navigation;
- does not rely exclusively on JavaScript navigation.

Google's current documentation explicitly recommends crawlable anchors and says every page you care about should be linked from at least one other site page.


## 03.91 — THE USER-JOURNEY HATER TEST

For every screen:


### “Where am I?”

Must be obvious.


### “Why am I seeing this?”

Must be obvious.


### “What am I supposed to decide?”

Must be obvious.


### “What happens if I click?”

Must be predictable.


### “Where does training actually happen?”

Must never be ambiguous.


### “Can I continue without understanding this animation?”

Yes.


### “Can I change my mind?”

Yes.


### “Can I use Back?”

Yes.


### “Can I do it with one thumb?”

Mobile: yes.


### “Can I do it with keyboard only?”

Yes.


## 03.92 — THE ART-DIRECTOR HATER TEST

Does the experience feel like:

generic gym website + fancy transitions?

If yes:

fail.

Does it use boxing metaphors literally everywhere?

If yes:

fail.

Does it confuse spectacle with difficulty?

If yes:

fail.

Does every visual mechanism grow from:

**starting point → routes → decision → destination?**

If yes:

we are getting somewhere.


## 03.93 — THE MOBILE-PROSPECT HATER TEST

Imagine:

4G one hand outdoors searching “MMA Colomiers” knows nothing about Boxing Center.

Within approximately the first meaningful interaction sequence:

they must understand:

BOXING CENTER ≠ PHYSICAL COLOMIERS CLUB BUT

BOXING CENTER CAN STILL SOLVE MY MMA NEED AND THIS SITE CAN TELL ME WHERE

Anything that delays that comprehension is suspect.


## 03.94 — THE GOOGLE/AI HATER TEST

A crawler lands on `/mma/`.

Can it answer:

- Does Boxing Center have a location in Colomiers?
- Does Boxing Center offer MMA accessible to Colomiers residents?
- Where?
- Which page owns this fact?
- What related pages exist?
- Where is the actual physical venue?
- Where is the official destination?

Without executing the selector?

It must.

The interactive layer enhances the semantic answer.

It never replaces it.


## 03.95 — THE PERFORMANCE HATER TEST

Disable all animation.

Does the page remain beautiful?

Disable JavaScript.

Does the primary journey remain intelligible?

Slow network.

Does useful HTML arrive before interactive enhancements?

Low-end phone.

Can the user still accomplish the job?

If not:

we designed a demo.

Not a website.


## 03.96 — PART 03 LOCKED EXPERIENCE SYSTEM

We now have a clear interaction hierarchy:

SEARCH ↓ LAND ↓ UNDERSTAND THE TRUTH ↓ IDENTIFY NEED ↓ DISCOVER DISCIPLINE ↓ COMPARE REAL CLUBS ↓ PLAN ↓ UNDERSTAND FIRST VISIT ↓ HAND OFF TO PHYSICAL CLUB

And our global site language becomes:

DISCIPLINES TROUVER MON CLUB PLANNINGS TARIFS COMMENCER

The homepage becomes:

QUESTION ↓ TRUTH ↓ INTENT ↓ DISCIPLINES ↓ PROXIMITY ENGINE ↓ REAL CLUBS ↓ BEGINNER REASSURANCE ↓ PLANNING ↓ EVIDENCE ↓ GO

This is now substantially different from:

hero → about → services → testimonials → CTA.


## 03.97 — PROJECT MECHANISM EMERGING

Parts 01–03 are beginning to converge on a larger design concept:


### THE ROUTE IS THE INTERFACE.

The website begins at **COLOMIERS**.

Every decision creates a direction.

Every discipline creates a branch.

Every verified club is a destination.

Every scroll removes branches until the visitor has one clear path.

This could eventually govern:

- graphic language;
- motion;
- navigation;
- page transitions;
- selector UI;
- typography placement;
- data visualization;
- loading states;
- 404;
- mobile navigation;
- even metadata/social imagery.

That is promising because it is not decoration attached after architecture.

**It is architecture becoming art direction.**

But we do not declare it the final visual system yet.

It still has to survive reference fusion and the +1 gate.


## 03.98 — LOCKED PART 03 PRINCIPLE

**Every interface element must reduce the distance between “I live in Colomiers and want to train” and “I know exactly where, when, why and how I should begin.”**

If it doesn't reduce that distance:

**it doesn't belong.**


<a id="part-04-page-system-homepage-architecture"></a>

# PART 04 — Page System & Homepage Architecture

Parts 01–03 gave us the strategy, search ownership, and journey.

Part 04 answers the next question:

**What exactly happens on the homepage, section by section, and why is every visible pixel there?**

The homepage must satisfy the formal brief—local discovery, Minimes + Portet orientation, disciplines, beginner reassurance and useful redirection—without pretending a physical Colomiers club exists. It must also use the supplied Boxing Center imagery responsibly across desktop and mobile rather than turning photography into decorative filler.

And one thing is now locked:


### THE ROUTE IS THE INTERFACE.

Not a literal Google Maps route.

A visual and behavioral grammar of:

**origin → branching → selection → convergence → destination.**


## 04.0 — THE HOMEPAGE MUST HAVE A SHAPE

Most gym websites have no narrative shape.

They are collections:

Hero Services About Numbers Testimonials CTA Footer

Our homepage must instead have a **directional arc**:

COLOMIERS ↓ UNCERTAINTY ↓ POSSIBILITIES ↓ SELF-SELECTION ↓ COMPARISON ↓ REAL PLACES ↓ REASSURANCE ↓ PRACTICALITY ↓ CERTAINTY ↓ GO

Visually, the page starts **open**.

Many possibilities.

By the bottom it becomes **resolved**.

One or two actions.

That convergence becomes part of the art direction.


## 04.1 — EXPERIENCE DENSITY

We deliberately do **not** make every section spectacular.

The current Lusion site is useful here: a strong immersive real-time statement is contained inside a comparatively restrained editorial system rather than every piece of content competing for spectacle. Locomotive similarly gets enormous impact from typography, timing and transition rather than filling every viewport with 3D. Igloo explicitly positions technology as something whose complexity should feel effortless. (Lusion)

Our intensity curve:

01 HERO ██████████ 02 TRUTH ███ 03 INTENT SELECTOR ████████ 04 DISCIPLINES █████ 05 PROXIMITY ENGINE ██████████ 06 REAL CLUBS ███████ 07 FIRST SESSION ████ 08 PLANNING ██████ 09 EVIDENCE ███ 10 FINAL CONVERGENCE ████████ 11 FOOTER ██

Two peaks:


### Peak A

Hero.


### Peak B

Proximity Engine.

Everything else supports them.

That prevents Baffled Bar from becoming sensory exhaustion.


## 04.2 — GLOBAL HOMEPAGE CANVAS

Before individual sections, lock the underlying structural grammar.


### Grid

Desktop:

**12-column editorial grid**

with deliberately asymmetrical compositions.

Not everything centered.

Not every section container `max-width: 1200px`.

Three content widths:

WIDE visual / tools / spatial compositions STANDARD major editorial layouts READING long-form copy / answers

Approximate conceptual values, not final tokens:

Wide 92–96vw Standard 82–88vw Reading 48–64rem

The final Design System will convert these into actual tokens.


## 04.3 — THE ROUTE SPINE

A recurring graphical element runs through the experience.

Call it provisionally:


### THE SPINE

It is not permanently visible as a big line down the page.

It can become:

- divider;
- progress indicator;
- motion path;
- connector;
- underline;
- card border;
- map-like relationship;

- section-entry marker;
- selected-state indicator.

This is important.

The route mechanism must become a **grammar**, not one hero trick.


## 04.4 — SPINE STATES

It has conceptual states:

ORIGIN

- UNRESOLVED
- ──────────── BRANCHING ┌────
- ───────┼──── └──── SELECTED
- ════════════
- ARRIVED
- ────────────
- COLOMIERS PORTET

Again: not necessarily rendered this literally.

The states govern motion/design.


## 04.5 — MATERIAL ATTITUDE

We don't choose final colors yet.

But the interface should avoid:

- generic neon gym graphics;
- grungy boxing textures everywhere;
- fake sweat;
- smoke overlays;
- sparks;
- chain-link backgrounds;
- red/black MMA cliché;
- metallic “fighter” fonts.

Our imagery already supplies aggression and physicality.

The interface should supply:

**clarity + intelligence + tension.**

That contrast is more sophisticated.


## 04.6 — PHOTOGRAPHY RULE

The brief asks for Minimes photos supplied by the team.

Critical rule:

**A Minimes image cannot visually imply “this is a gym in Colomiers.”**

Therefore any image whose location matters receives context.

For example:

**BOXING CENTER MINIMES · TOULOUSE**

not:

**COLOMIERS**

Photography can illustrate:

- boxing culture;
- training;
- disciplines;
- coach/member interaction.

But location-specific facility claims must remain labeled.

This eliminates a subtle but serious form of geographic deception.


## 04.7 — HOMEPAGE MASTER STRUCTURE

**ID Section Primary job**

H01 Hero / Origin Establish concept and utility

H02 Truth Kill geographic ambiguity

H03 Intent Selector Turn visitor into a profile

H04 Discipline Spectrum Explain available paths

H05 Proximity Engine Generate useful recommendation

H06 Real Destinations Materialize Minimes + Portet

H07 First Session Remove emotional friction

H08 Planning Window Remove schedule friction

H09 Evidence Establish credibility

H10 Convergence Reduce choice to next action

H11 Footer Knowledge map + entity truth

Now each one.


### H01 — HERO: THE ORIGIN


## 04.8 — Job

Within seconds:

1. recognize Colomiers; 2. understand Boxing Center can be relevant; 3. understand the page helps choose;

4. see a direct action.

Do not explain the entire network.

Do not show four offers.

Do not show testimonials.

Do not show prices.


## 04.9 — Proposed content hierarchy

Eyebrow:

**BOXING CENTER · DEPUIS COLOMIERS**

Hero:


### **TU PARS DE COLOMIERS.

LE BON CLUB DÉPEND DE CE QUE TU CHERCHES.**

Supporting statement:

Boxe anglaise, MMA, remise en forme, enfants : trouve la discipline et le Boxing Center qui correspondent réellement à ton besoin.

Primary:

**TROUVER MON CLUB**

Secondary:

**EXPLORER LES DISCIPLINES**

Microtruth:

Les entraînements ont lieu dans les clubs physiques du réseau, notamment Minimes et Portet-sur-Garonne.

That last sentence is semantically important.


## 04.10 — Composition

Desktop:

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────┐ │ BC / COLOMIERS NAV CTA │ │ │ │ [ROUTE / LIVING SYSTEM] │ │ │ │ TU PARS DE COLOMIERS

- │ │ COLOMIERS. │ │ ╲ │ │ LE BON CLUB ╲ BOXE │ │ DÉPEND DE... ╲ MMA │ │ ╲ ENFANTS │ │ │ │ [TROUVER MON CLUB] │ └─────────────────────────────────────────────┘

Not literal final art.

Structural intention:

```text
type and route coexist, rather than text layered over full-screen boxer photography.
```


## 04.11 — Hero visual mechanism

Potentially the page's main real-time mechanism.

An abstract route-field.

Not a map.

Imagine:

- Colomiers represented by one anchor;
- multiple fine paths exist around it;
- paths react very subtly to pointer;
- each corresponds conceptually to an intent;

- scrolling progressively creates order.

Think **decision topology**, not GPS.

Lusion is relevant here for the idea of one strong interactive object becoming the hero rather than using real-time 3D as background noise. (Lusion)


## 04.12 — Hero +1

Reference mechanisms can give us real-time graphics.

Our +1:

**THE VISUAL DOES NOT REPRESENT A PRODUCT. IT REPRESENTS THE USER'S UNRESOLVED DECISION.**

The graphic changes meaning as the user interacts later.

This is critical.

At the top: unresolved network.

Later: branches disappear.

At the recommendation: one route remains.

Therefore the hero visual has **stateful narrative memory**.

That is significantly stronger than decorative 3D.


## 04.13 — Hero motion

Sequence:

**0–300 ms** UI is already readable.

**300–900 ms** origin node resolves.

**600–1400 ms** route candidates form.

**900–1500 ms** headline/subtitle settle.

No preloader.

No intro requiring completion.

Cursor interaction optional.

Scroll instantly available.


## 04.14 — Reduced motion

No route drawing animation.

Final route topology displays immediately.

No loss of information.


## 04.15 — Mobile transformation

Do **not** show a tiny version of a desktop interactive field.

Mobile:

BOXING CENTER DEPUIS COLOMIERS TU CHERCHES QUOI ? BOXE MMA FORME ENFANT

────────

- COLOMIERS [TROUVER MON CLUB]

The utility enters sooner.

The route visualization becomes simpler and perhaps touch-reactive only after choice.

Same conceptual richness.

Different composition.


## 04.16 — Semantics

```text
<header>
<nav>...</nav>
</header>
<main>
<section aria-labelledby="hero-title">
<p>Boxing Center · Depuis Colomiers</p>
<h1 id="hero-title">...</h1>
<p>...</p>
<a href="/quel-club/">Trouver mon club</a>
<a href="#disciplines">Explorer les disciplines</a>
</section>
```

Visual canvas/WebGL is decorative from accessibility standpoint unless it conveys an interactive choice.

If decorative:

`aria-hidden="true"`.

The textual graph provides semantic meaning.


### H02 — TRUTH: THE CONTRACT


## 04.17 — Job

Destroy ambiguity early.

This section should feel almost shockingly straightforward after the hero.


## 04.18 — Copy territory

Large statement:


### PAS DE SALLE BOXING CENTER PHYSIQUE À COLOMIERS.

Smaller continuation:


### CE SITE EXISTE POUR T'ÉVITER DE CHERCHER AU MAUVAIS ENDROIT.

Explanation:

Il compare les solutions Boxing Center pertinentes depuis Colomiers et t'oriente vers les clubs physiques adaptés à ta discipline, notamment Toulouse Minimes et Portet-sur-Garonne.

This directly protects the formal requirement.


## 04.19 — Composition

Quiet.

No big photography.

Large whitespace.

Strong typography.

Potentially one route entering the section and stopping at:

**COLOMIERS**

then splitting toward actual destinations below.

We want the factual statement to land.


## 04.20 — +1

Most sites bury limitations.

We make the limitation the **brand trust moment**.

The user realizes:

“They're not trying to trick me.”

This creates a credibility dividend that competitors physically in Colomiers may not need—but we do.


### H03 — INTENT SELECTOR: WHO ARE YOU HERE TO BECOME?


## 04.21 — Job

Convert generic traffic into explicit intent.

This is the site's first meaningful interaction.


## 04.22 — Initial prompt


### POURQUOI TU VEUX METTRE LES GANTS ?

Better than:

Choisis ta discipline.

Because many visitors don't yet know their discipline.

Options:


### APPRENDRE À BOXER

“I want technique.”


### ESSAYER LE MMA

“I want a complete combat sport.”


### ME REMETTRE EN FORME

“I want physical benefit.”


### POUR MON ENFANT

“I am a parent.”


### PASSER UN CAP

Experienced/competitive intent.


### JE NE SAIS PAS ENCORE

Needs orientation.


## 04.23 — Interaction

Options are **large typographic rows**, not six SaaS cards.

Desktop:

01 APPRENDRE À BOXER → 02 ESSAYER LE MMA → 03 ME REMETTRE EN FORME → 04 TROUVER POUR MON ENFANT → 05 PASSER UN CAP → 06 JE NE SAIS PAS ENCORE →

Hover/touch causes the route spine to branch toward the associated discipline.

Photography may change subtly alongside.


## 04.24 — Example branch

Select:

**ME REMETTRE EN FORME**

Next state:

**QU'EST-CE QUI COMPTE LE PLUS ?**

- cardio;
- technique;
- non-competition;
- women-specific group;
- variety.

No more than two decision levels on homepage.

Anything deeper moves into `/quel-club/`.


## 04.25 — +1

The selector does not merely filter content.

**It physically edits the website's route system.**

After selection:

- irrelevant branches reduce opacity;
- relevant sections become subtly tagged;
- future recommendations inherit context.

The interface literally becomes **your version** of the homepage.

Without account/login.


## 04.26 — Persistence

Use lightweight client state.

Potentially:

sessionStorage

or explicit query state.

Never required for basic navigation.

Always:

**CHANGER**

available.


## 04.27 — Accessibility

Use actual radio/button semantics.

No div-click handlers.

Selection confirmation exposed textually:

Objectif sélectionné : remise en forme.

Dynamic result region receives an appropriate live announcement, but avoid chatty `aria-live` spam.


### H04 — DISCIPLINE SPECTRUM: THE PATHS


## 04.28 — Job

Now that intent exists, explain the real disciplines.

This should not become a “services grid.”


## 04.29 — Structure

Four main worlds:

BOXE ANGLAISE MMA BOXING FITNESS ENFANTS

Within each:

- sensory definition;
- beginner threshold;
- relevant audience;
- actual club availability;
- next route.


## 04.30 — Visual approach

A **continuous horizontal/diagonal editorial field** rather than four detached rectangles.

Imagine the route spine crossing different visual territories.

Each discipline interrupts the grid differently.


### Boxe anglaise

Large portrait/action photography, precise typography.


### MMA

More spatial framing, floor/cage geometry if real assets exist.


### Fitness

Movement/repetition/rhythm.


### Children

Warmer human interaction; never infantilizing cartoon style.


## 04.31 — Photography rule

Actual images should be labeled when location-specific.

Example:

**BOXE ANGLAISE · BOXING CENTER MINIMES**

This simultaneously strengthens credibility and avoids geographic confusion.


## 04.32 — Copy structure

Each discipline gets approximately:

NAME ONE-SENTENCE HUMAN DESCRIPTION BEGINNER STATUS PRIMARY BENEFIT WHERE AVAILABLE [EXPLORE]

No 300-word card.

Depth lives on route page.


## 04.33 — Interaction

Pointer movement can reveal secondary information.

But default state must already contain enough to understand each discipline.

No “mystery card” whose text appears only on hover.


## 04.34 — Mobile

Vertical cinematic index.

Each discipline gets one strong visual slice.

Sticky mini-index possible:

BOXE · MMA · FORME · ENFANTS

but only if usable without crowding.


## 04.35 — +1

Because the user selected intent in H03, this section can **reorder emphasis**.

If intent = child:

Children appears first / receives strongest emphasis.

If intent = MMA:

MMA receives emphasis.

DOM/search content remains stable.

Visual priority adapts without hiding other content.

The site's editorial hierarchy responds to the visitor.


### H05 — PROXIMITY ENGINE: THE SIGNATURE

This is the homepage's strongest functional moment.


## 04.36 — Job

Answer:

**Given what I want, which actual club makes sense?**

If this mechanism is weak, our entire anti-doorway strategy weakens.


## 04.37 — Section entrance

After multiple branches:

The route spine becomes chaotic again.

Then:


### **ON A LES DISCIPLINES.

MAINTENANT, OÙ VAS-TU ?**


## 04.38 — Inputs

Only meaningful criteria.

Potential initial version:


### WHAT

discipline/goal


### WHEN

broad availability


### LEVEL

first time / already practise


### FOR WHOM

adult / child where relevant

Avoid asking things we cannot operationally use.

No fake personalization.


## 04.39 — Result

Example only; live data must decide:

TON PARCOURS COLOMIERS ↓ MMA ↓ DÉBUTANT ↓ SOIR ↓ PORTET-SUR-GARONNE

BOXING CENTER PORTET Why this result: ✓ MMA offered ✓ relevant programme ✓ beginner-compatible ✓ compatible time option [VOIR LE CLUB] [CHANGER MES CRITÈRES]


## 04.40 — Never rank arbitrary preferences

Do not say:

**BEST CLUB**

Say:

**OPTION LA PLUS COHÉRENTE AVEC TES CRITÈRES**

unless evidence genuinely supports a deterministic best option.


## 04.41 — Result explanation

Visible.

Not hidden behind info icon.

This is one of the biggest trust mechanisms.


## 04.42 — Data architecture requirement

Recommendation engine cannot contain manually duplicated claims.

It reads from verified club data.

Conceptually:

getClubMatch({ discipline, level, availability, audience }, clubRegistry)

The eventual Implementation Contract specifies exact fields.


## 04.43 — Visual climax

This is where the route interface finally **converges**.

All unused paths fall away.

One thick/clear path remains.

Destination becomes physically legible.

This is our equivalent of an award-site spectacle.

But spectacle = **information resolving**.

Not a spaceship floating around.


## 04.44 — Real-time graphics?

Potentially.

But only if technically justified.

Possible implementations from lightest to heaviest:

1. SVG path + CSS/WAAPI; 2. Canvas 2D;

3. WebGL; 4. custom shader/3D.

We choose the cheapest solution that achieves the artistic goal.

Darkroom's current open-source work is useful philosophically here: creative motion needs disciplined timing/render infrastructure, not random independent animation loops. (Darkroom OSS)

Astro implementation should therefore centralize any interactive render loop rather than creating multiple uncontrolled per-component RAF loops.


## 04.45 — Mobile engine

Should arguably be **better** than desktop.

One question per view:

1 / 3 TU VEUX FAIRE QUOI ? MMA BOXE FORME ENFANT

Swipe not required.

Tap.

Then:

2 / 3 QUAND TU PEUX ?

Then result.

No tiny multi-column form.


## 04.46 — No-match

Already locked:

**PAS DE CORRESPONDANCE EXACTE.**

Then actual alternatives.

Never fabricate a club recommendation to avoid an empty state.


## 04.47 — +1

This is the project-level **Proximity as Product** mechanism made tangible.

External references give us spatial interaction.

Our own +1 is:

**A search landing page becomes a transparent recommendation system built from verified physical-club truth.**

That is materially different from the references and from our existing sites.


### H06 — REAL DESTINATIONS: THE PLACES BECOME PHYSICAL


## 04.48 — Job

After algorithmic/orientation logic, remind the user:

these are real places with actual rooms, coaches, equipment and addresses.

Humanize the recommendation.


## 04.49 — Entrance


### LE CHEMIN FINIT DANS UNE VRAIE SALLE.

Then:

**MINIMES**

and

**PORTET-SUR-GARONNE**


## 04.50 — Avoid identical location cards

I want each destination to have its own editorial composition based on actual difference.

For example, subject to verified registry:


### MINIMES

History / English-boxing character / real physical assets.


### PORTET

Multi-combat footprint / MMA/cage / broader combat mix.

These are not decorative brand stories.

They help decide.


## 04.51 — Layout

Desktop could use an asymmetrical **split world**:

MINIMES PORTET [large image] text text [large image] verified facts verified facts [VISITER MINIMES ↗] [VISITER PORTET ↗]

The dividing route line becomes the comparison axis.


## 04.52 — Location truth

Actual:

- city;
- address;
- relevant disciplines;
- relevant equipment;
- source/freshness where appropriate.

Never:

**14 MINUTES FROM COLOMIERS**

unless a routing system can prove it for the visitor's origin/time.


## 04.53 — Mobile

Do not stack two giant equivalent cards.

Start with:

**CE QUI COMPTE POUR TOI**

and show decisive differences.

Then two destination profiles.


## 04.54 — +1

The comparison is not “features.”

It's **decision evidence**.

Any attribute displayed must answer:

“Would this change which club I choose?”

If no:

remove it.


### H07 — FIRST SESSION: THE HUMAN RELEASE

After the dense decision engine, we need emotional decompression.


## 04.55 — Job

Remove intimidation.

Your previous Portet/Minimes/Saint-Cyprien work already showed this is a strong internal mechanism.

Here we compress its best form.


## 04.56 — Section tone

Less interface.

More human.

Potential large real training image.

Copy:


### **TU SAIS OÙ ALLER.

MAINTENANT, VOILÀ CE QUI SE PASSE QUAND TU ENTRES.**


## 04.57 — Sequence

Use 4–5 steps.

No complicated timeline UI.

Potential:

01 TU ARRIVES 02 ON T'ORIENTE 03 TU T'ÉCHAUFFES 04 TU APPRENDS 05 TU DÉCIDES SI TU REVIENS

Only verified factual details.


## 04.58 — Visual mechanism

Scrolling can progressively crop or reveal **one real sequence**, rather than five unrelated stock images.

If assets permit:

one continuous photographic contact sheet / strip.

That gives physical continuity.


## 04.59 — Mobile

Natural vertical story.

No horizontal carousel.


## 04.60 — +1

If the visitor has already selected a club:

the copy can become context-aware:

**TA PREMIÈRE SÉANCE À PORTET**

with actual relevant information.

If no selection:

general network-safe wording.

Again: personalization without account.


### H08 — PLANNING WINDOW: TIME BECOMES A ROUTE


## 04.61 — Job

Convert interest into feasible behavior.


## 04.62 — Headline


### LE BON CLUB, C'EST AUSSI CELUI OÙ TU PEUX VRAIMENT VENIR.

Then:

**QUAND ES-TU DISPONIBLE ?**


## 04.63 — Homepage version

Do not dump full weekly schedule.

Select:

- discipline;
- broad day/time.

Return a few compatible verified options.

Then:

**VOIR TOUS LES PLANNINGS**


## 04.64 — Freshness

Visible:

Planning vérifié le …

if our data pipeline can support it.

This becomes part of the design.


## 04.65 — Visual

The route line becomes a **time axis**.

Nice fusion:

Spatial path → temporal path.

Same graphical grammar.

New meaning.


## 04.66 — +1

Instead of creating a new unrelated schedule component, the design system transforms the same route primitive from:

**where?**

into:

**when?**

That creates deep visual coherence.


### H09 — EVIDENCE: NO MARKETING ADJECTIVES


## 04.67 — Job

Answer:

Why should I trust any of this?


## 04.68 — No generic benefits

Forbidden:

**PASSION EXPERTISE CONVIVIALITÉ**

unless supported with something meaningful.

Use real proof:

- coach qualifications;
- real facilities;
- real club history;
- federation affiliation;
- real reviews associated with actual location;
- verified programme breadth;
- real photography.


## 04.69 — Composition

Potentially an editorial **evidence ledger**.

Example:

10 ANS network history ────────────── 5 CLUBS actual network footprint ────────────── FFBOXE · FFKMDA · ... verified affiliations ──────────────

Numbers only if current/verified.

No animated zero counters.

One of the exact defects from our own-work review was counters exposing `0` before JS; that pattern is banned.


## 04.70 — Reviews

If shown:

BOXING CENTER MINIMES Google rating… source/date

Separate from:

BOXING CENTER PORTET ...

Never synthesize a fictional Colomiers score.


## 04.71 — +1

Trust information is integrated into the recommendation architecture.

Example:

If a recommendation says Portet due to MMA:

show relevant evidence right there.

H09 becomes broader corroboration, not “social proof pasted near footer.”


### H10 — FINAL CONVERGENCE: GO


## 04.72 — Job

At the beginning we had branches.

At the end we want at most three meaningful actions.


## 04.73 — Copy


### **AU DÉBUT, TU CHERCHAIS “BOXE À COLOMIERS”.

MAINTENANT, TU SAIS OÙ COMMENCER.**

This is powerful because it acknowledges the original search journey.

Then, depending on user state:


### Selected club exists


### BOXING CENTER PORTET

**VOIR LE CLUB ↗**

**PRÉPARER MA PREMIÈRE SÉANCE**

**CHANGER MON CHOIX**


### No state


### IL RESTE UNE DÉCISION.

**TROUVER MON CLUB**

**PRÉPARER MA PREMIÈRE SÉANCE**

**POSER UNE QUESTION**


## 04.74 — Visual

All route branches have disappeared.

One line.

One destination.

The homepage visually completes its thesis.


## 04.75 — Motion

Very restrained.

Convergence should feel satisfying, not celebratory.

No confetti.

No giant exploding glove.


## 04.76 — +1

The page remembers what the user resolved and ends accordingly.

The final CTA is not universally predefined.

It's the **consequence of the journey**.


### H11 — FOOTER: THE ENTITY MAP


## 04.77 — Job

Navigation, machine clarity and factual closure.


## 04.78 — Structure

BOXING CENTER DEPUIS COLOMIERS PRATIQUER Boxe anglaise MMA Boxing Fitness Boxe enfants S'ORIENTER Trouver mon club Première séance Plannings Tarifs CLUBS PHYSIQUES Minimes ↗ Portet-sur-Garonne ↗ AIDE Contact Téléphone LÉGAL Mentions légales Confidentialité

Truth statement:

**Boxingcenter-colomiers.fr est un service d'orientation locale Boxing Center. Aucune salle physique Boxing Center n'est actuellement implantée à Colomiers.**


## 04.79 — Visual treatment

Quiet.

Architectural.

Potential route spine terminates here and exposes the network relationship clearly.

No giant promotional footer CTA necessary because H10 already converted the journey.


## 04.80 — HOMEPAGE VISUAL RHYTHM

Full flow:

H01 SPATIAL / EXPANSIVE ↓ H02 TYPOGRAPHIC / QUIET ↓ H03 INTERACTIVE / DENSE ↓ H04 EDITORIAL / IMAGE ↓ H05 INTERACTIVE / CLIMAX ↓ H06 PHYSICAL / IMAGE ↓ H07 HUMAN / QUIET ↓ H08 UTILITY / DATA ↓ H09 EVIDENCE / QUIET ↓ H10 SPATIAL / CONVERGENCE ↓ H11 INFORMATION / REST

This prevents monotony.


## 04.81 — SECTION HEIGHT RULE

No blanket:

section { min-height: 100vh; }

Approximate experiential categories:


### Hero

Near viewport scale.


### Truth

Short.


### Selector

Variable based on interaction.


### Disciplines

Long editorial section.


### Engine

Large but not artificially pinned forever.


### Destinations

Medium/large.


### First session

Medium.


### Planning

Medium.


### Evidence

Compact.


### Final

Medium.


## 04.82 — SCROLL PINNING

Use sparingly.

Possible candidate:

Proximity Engine.

But even there:

maximum one controlled sticky sequence rather than 4000px scroll-jacking.

No section should require twenty wheel gestures to reveal four sentences.


## 04.83 — PHOTO QUOTA

A key anti-slop rule:

Every image must have one of four jobs:

1. **proof** — real facility/person; 2. **orientation** — shows which club; 3. **emotion** — communicates practice; 4. **instruction** — demonstrates what happens.

If an image does none:

remove it.

No decorative stock athlete merely because blank space scares us.


## 04.84 — COPY QUOTA

Each section must have:


### one dominant idea;


### one supporting explanation;


### one next action.

If a section has five messages:

split or delete.

SEO depth belongs on dedicated pages and lower-detail layers.

Homepage remains decisive.


## 04.85 — SEO ROLE OF HOMEPAGE

Homepage owns broad local intent.

Therefore it needs enough crawlable text to establish:

- Colomiers context;
- no physical Colomiers location;
- Boxing Center;
- Minimes;
- Portet;
- boxe;
- MMA;
- fitness;
- children;
- nearby practice;
- beginner suitability.

But those concepts are distributed through meaningful sections.

No giant bottom-of-page “SEO text.”


## 04.86 — GEO ROLE

An answer engine reading only static HTML must understand:

Boxing Center does not have a physical location in Colomiers.

This website helps Colomiers residents identify relevant nearby Boxing Center clubs.

The two major destinations being presented are Minimes and Portet-sur-Garonne.

Different disciplines may make one location more appropriate.

That answer must exist independently of animation/selector state.


## 04.87 — ACCESSIBILITY CONTRACT FOR HOMEPAGE

Every section receives a meaningful heading.

Interactive route visualization never owns essential text.

Reduced-motion state is intentionally designed.

Keyboard user can complete club selection.

Focus states belong visually to the art direction.

No focus outline deletion.

No invisible skip link.

Meaningful image alts.

Decorative route graphics hidden from assistive tech.

Color never solely communicates selection.

Dynamic recommendation includes textual justification.

Touch targets are generous.

Zoom/reflow does not destroy comparison interfaces.


## 04.88 — PERFORMANCE CONTRACT

Aiming toward later concrete budgets, but architecture already says:


### Critical path

HTML + essential CSS + hero typography first.


### Photography

responsive AVIF/WebP sources.


### Route visual

progressive enhancement.


### Proximity engine

hydrated island only where needed.


### No global React app

Astro remains the shell.


### WebGL

if eventually justified, dynamically loaded after essential experience—not a dependency for first content paint.


### Fonts

small, deliberately selected family/weights.


### Below fold

lazy load responsibly.


## 04.89 — ASTRO ISLAND STRATEGY

Tentatively:

```text
Astro static/server HTML
│
├── Hero visual optional small interactive island
├── Intent selector interactive island
├── Proximity engine interactive island
├── Planning filter interactive island
└── Navigation minimal JS where necessary
```

Everything else:

Astro/HTML/CSS first.

No hydrating an entire 11-section homepage because three sections move.


## 04.90 — INTERNAL REFERENCE FUSION

Our own previous work contributes several mechanisms:


### Saint-Cyprien

physical-space journey / progressive discovery.


### Minimes

planning clarity + first-session architecture.


### Ramonville

**truth as spectacle**—when an asset/fact is absent, expose reality intelligently instead of inventing it.


### Portet

narrative conversion and beginner anxiety removal.


### Boutique

transactional hierarchy.


### Concours

step/round progression.


### Blagnac

strict local non-claims.

We are not cloning any of their layouts.

**We are promoting their strongest mechanisms into a shared system.**


## 04.91 — EXTERNAL FUSION LEDGER

This is required by Fusion Baffled.

**Surface Borrowed**

**layer**

**Reference Operation Becomes**

**here**

**+1**

Hero Singular immersive statement

Lusion (Lusion) inversion +

subtraction

Interactive visualization of unresolved choice instead of portfolio object

Visual state changes meaning later in journey

Global type/motion

Type as major spatial actor

Locomotive (Locomotive)

graft + recontextualization

Typography behaves like directional signage

Type participates in orientation rather than spectacle

Interaction philosophy

Technology feels effortless

Igloo (Igloo) compression Complexity hidden behind a few clear choices

Recommendatio n transparency exposes logic only when useful

Motion engine

Unified performant motion discipline

Darkroom OSS (Darkroom OSS)

technical graft Shared timing/render infrastructure

Same route primitive transforms spatial → temporal → progress meaning

Homepage journey

Progressive physical-spa ce narrative

Internal Saint-Cyprien

escalation Whole homepage becomes an orientation journey

State persists across sections

First-sessio n

Anxiety-remo val storytelling

Internal Portet/Minimes

compression Short emotional release after engine

Content adapts to selected destination

Truth Documentary honesty

inversion “No Colomiers gym” becomes hero-level proposition

Absence itself creates the product

Planning Schedule as utility

Internal Minimes

collision Club comparison + availability

Time uses same visual route grammar

This is already more than “mix three pretty websites.”


## 04.92 — ANTI-CLONE TEST

Could someone look at this homepage and say:

“It's just Lusion but for boxing”?

No.

If they can:

fail.

Lusion contributes **singular immersive focus**, not composition.

Locomotive contributes **type confidence/timing**, not typography itself.

Igloo contributes **effortless complexity**, not visual identity.

Our existing sites contribute operational mechanisms.

The visible result is anchored in:

**Colomiers → decision → real club.**


## 04.93 — REJECTED HOMEPAGE PATTERNS

Explicitly forbidden:


### Generic full-bleed athlete hero

Too easy.


### Four glowing discipline cards

SaaS/gym template.


### Animated counters at hero

Meaningless and previous implementation risk.


### Endless horizontal carousels

Weak mobile/accessibility default.


### 3D boxing glove floating in space

Decorative novelty.


### Boxing ring as literal navigation

Too thematic.


### Full map as hero

Makes the site look like logistics/Google Maps.


### Sticky purchase button

Wrong product role.


### Promo banner

Formal brief explicitly excludes aggressive promotions at this stage.


### Generic testimonial carousel

Not our trust model.


### SEO wall

No.


## 04.94 — HOMEPAGE “BAFFLED” TEST

Someone should leave remembering:

“That site asked what I wanted, visually narrowed the possibilities, explained exactly why a certain real club made sense, and the whole page seemed to physically turn into the route I chose.”

Not:

“Cool boxing site.”

The first is a **reference mechanism**.

The second is aesthetic competence.

We need the first.


## 04.95 — THE HOMEPAGE +1, FORMALLY NAMED

We now have enough to name it.


### THE RESOLVING ROUTE

Definition:

A persistent visual-information system begins as multiple unresolved paths from Colomiers. User intent and verified club data progressively remove possibilities until one actionable path remains. The same primitive governs hero art, selection, comparison, scheduling, page progress and final conversion.

Why this matters:

It does not exist merely as motion.

It connects:

**design UX SEO truth GEO truth data personalization conversion.**

That is Baffled Bar territory because the visual mechanism is inseparable from the website's actual job.


## 04.96 — PART 04 LOCKED HOMEPAGE

The homepage is now:

H01 THE ORIGIN Colomiers + unresolved paths H02 THE CONTRACT No fake physical Colomiers location H03 THE INTENT Why do you want to train? H04 THE PATHS Disciplines become understandable H05 THE RESOLUTION Verified recommendation engine H06 THE DESTINATIONS Minimes + Portet become physical H07 THE HUMAN MOMENT What happens first time H08 THE TIME When can this actually happen? H09 THE EVIDENCE Why trust the network/data? H10 THE CONVERGENCE One next action H11 THE ENTITY MAP Footer closes factual architecture

And the page-wide narrative is:


### FROM SEARCH QUERY TO PHYSICAL CERTAINTY.

That is now the homepage architecture I would lock.


<a id="part-05-visual-design-system"></a>

# PART 05 — Visual Design System


## 05.0 — PURPOSE

Part 04 established the homepage architecture.

Part 05 answers:

**What visual language can make “The Resolving Route” recognizable before the visitor has even understood how it works?**

This is not a moodboard.

This is the visual constitution that every future page, component, state, breakpoint, animation and asset must obey.

The objective is not:

“make Boxing Center look premium.”

The objective is:

**create a visual system so coherent that the interface itself appears to understand where the visitor is going.**

The system must simultaneously feel:

- physical;
- disciplined;
- editorial;
- contemporary;
- geographically intelligent;
- unmistakably Boxing Center;
- accessible;
- performant;
- non-template;
- non-AI-generated;
- credible enough for serious boxers;
- unintimidating enough for beginners and parents.

And it must preserve the brief's requirement that supplied Boxing Center imagery be web-optimized, responsive and semantically described rather than merely dropped into layouts.


## 05.1 — ART-DIRECTION THESIS

The central visual thesis is:


### PRECISION UNDER PRESSURE

Boxing provides the pressure.

The interface provides the precision.

We deliberately avoid trying to make every part of the interface feel “aggressive.”

That is too obvious.

Instead:

```text
photography = physical intensity
```

while

```text
interface = extreme control
```

The tension between the two creates sophistication.


## 05.2 — THE VISUAL PARADOX

The website should simultaneously feel:

RAW CONTROLLED PHYSICAL INTELLIGENT FAST PRECISE SPORT EDITORIAL LOCAL SYSTEMIC POWERFUL CALM

If we resolve this tension entirely toward either side, we lose.

Too physical:

→ generic MMA/gym website.

Too controlled:

→ SaaS application.

The identity lives in the collision.


## 05.3 — WHAT WE ARE NOT BUILDING

Explicit visual exclusions:

- no black/red “fight night” template;
- no smoky arena backgrounds;
- no flying particles;
- no sparks;
- no flames;
- no blood textures;
- no chain-link fence overlays;
- no carbon fibre;
- no faux metal;
- no boxing-glove icon wallpaper;
- no graffiti unless the actual brand context demands it;
- no giant muscular model staring at camera as entire concept;
- no “cyber” neon;
- no glowing cards;
- no glassmorphism;
- no purple-blue gradients;
- no bento-grid SaaS structure;
- no 24px-radius everything;
- no endless pills;
- no fake HUD;
- no generic red CTA everywhere;
- no AI-generated combat imagery used as documentary proof.

If the website could plausibly be reskinned into:

CrossFit crypto streetwear gaming MMA supplement brand

by changing the logo alone,

the visual system has failed.


## 05.4 — REFERENCE PRINCIPLE

External references are used by mechanism.

Lusion currently frames its work around distinctive interactive storytelling combining design, motion, 3D and development; the useful lesson for us is **concentrated immersive impact**, not copying its visual style.

Locomotive explicitly describes its approach as combining design thinking and technical know-how to create digital destinations; our useful layer is the integration of typography, development and art direction rather than treating “creative development” as decoration after UI design.

Igloo's current principle of “effortless complexity” is particularly relevant: the technical intelligence beneath the route system should be visible through usefulness, not technical clutter.

Darkroom's current tooling similarly emphasizes high-performance primitives, including unified animation-loop infrastructure, which reinforces the later implementation principle that visual sophistication must be systematized rather than composed from dozens of isolated animation hacks.

These are mechanism references.

None supplies our final aesthetic.


## 05.5 — BRAND INVARIANTS

The following are not playground elements.


### Official Boxing Center logo

Immutable.

Never:

- redraw;
- approximate;
- retype;
- generate;
- stretch;
- compress;
- modify spiral proportions;
- add unapproved embossing;
- recolor arbitrarily;
- place over backgrounds that destroy legibility.

If an official monochrome variant exists, use it.

If it does not exist:

do not invent one merely because a composition wants it.


## 05.6 — BOXING CENTER NAVY

The established network navy remains a foundational color.

Current system anchor:
