Google's current guidance continues to emphasize substantial, original, people-first content that helps someone achieve their goal rather than content written primarily to attract search traffic.

That is exactly our standard.


## 09.15 — MICROCOPY

Purpose:

remove tiny moments of uncertainty.

Examples:

Planning vérifié le 7 septembre 2026.

MMA sélectionné.

Aucun créneau vérifié pour ce choix.

Voir le planning officiel de Portet ↗

Changer mes critères.

Microcopy is not decorative personality.

It performs a job.


## 09.16 — H1 GRAMMAR

H1s should usually belong to one of three structures.


### QUESTION

Strong for search/problem intent.

Quelle boxe pour ton enfant quand tu habites à Colomiers ?


### DIRECT SITUATION

Faire de la boxe anglaise quand on habite à Colomiers.


### PROBLEM → ANSWER

Tu cherches du MMA depuis Colomiers ? Voilà ce que Boxing Center peut réellement te proposer.


## 09.17 — H1 RULES

H1 must:

- accurately summarize page purpose;
- remain human;
- reflect search context naturally;
- avoid repeating title mechanically;
- avoid promotional exaggeration;
- exist once as the main page heading.

Google specifically recommends descriptive, helpful page titles/headings rather than exaggerated or search-engine-first wording.


## 09.18 — SECTION HEADING GRAMMAR

Headings should generally be:


### Questions

**EST-CE QU'ON PREND DES COUPS QUAND ON DÉBUTE ?**


### Decisions

**MINIMES OU PORTET POUR LA BOXE ANGLAISE ?**


### Truths

**LE MMA NE COMMENCE PAS PAR UN COMBAT.**


### Actions

**CHOISIS CE QUE TU VEUX TRAVAILLER.**

Avoid:

NOS SERVICES

NOS AVANTAGES

POURQUOI NOUS CHOISIR

unless they genuinely describe the section better—which they rarely will.


## 09.19 — NO “NOS…”

The Colomiers site should use **“nos”** carefully.

Why?

Because:

nos salles à Colomiers

would be false.

Allowed:

les clubs du réseau Boxing Center

Better than:

nos clubs proches de Colomiers

when geographic/entity clarity is important.


## 09.20 — GEOGRAPHIC LANGUAGE SYSTEM


### PREFERRED

- depuis Colomiers;
- près de Colomiers;
- à proximité de Colomiers;
- quand tu habites à Colomiers;
- accessible depuis Colomiers;
- les pratiquants venant de Colomiers;
- le club du réseau pertinent depuis Colomiers.


### USE CAREFULLY

- boxe à Colomiers;
- MMA à Colomiers.

These may appear in:

questions/search framing:

Tu cherches où faire du MMA à Colomiers ou à proximité ?

But the answer must immediately clarify physical reality.


### FORBIDDEN

- notre salle de Colomiers;
- notre club à Colomiers;
- nos coachs à Colomiers;
- nos cours de Colomiers;
- Boxing Center situé à Colomiers;
- adresse Boxing Center Colomiers.

The original brief explicitly prohibits creating that impression.


## 09.21 — LOCAL COPY SHOULD SOUND LOCAL WITHOUT FAKE LOCALISM

Bad:

Au cœur de Colomiers…

We are not there.

Bad:

Votre club de quartier.

False.

Better:

Tu habites Colomiers et tu cherches où boxer ?

Local relevance comes from the **user's origin**.

Not from pretending the business shares it.


## 09.22 — DISCIPLINE TERMINOLOGY

Use correct discipline names consistently.

Canonical names:

Boxe anglaise MMA Grappling Jiu-jitsu brésilien / JJB Kick-boxing Boxing Fitness Cardio Boxing Boxing Lady Baby Boxe Boxe éducative Préparation physique

Exact capitalization should be standardized globally.


## 09.23 — NO TERMINOLOGY MIXING

Do not alternate randomly:

`kickboxing`

`kick boxing`

`Kick Boxing`

kick-boxing

Pick one canonical public form.

Likewise:

`MMA` always uppercase.

`JJB` consistently uppercase.


## 09.24 — EXPLAIN SPECIALIST TERMS ON FIRST USE

Example:

Le grappling est un travail de lutte et de contrôle au sol sans frappes.

Then later:

grappling

alone is fine.

Do not assume beginner knowledge.


## 09.25 — BEGINNER VOICE

The beginner voice must remove shame.

Never:

Même si tu n'es pas sportif…

This can sound condescending.

Better:

Tu peux commencer sans expérience préalable.

Never:

N'aie pas peur.

It can introduce fear.

Better:

Une première séance commence par des bases adaptées à ton niveau.


## 09.26 — BEGINNER QUESTIONS TO ANSWER DIRECTLY

We should explicitly address:

- Do I need experience?
- Do I need to be fit?
- Do I need gloves?
- Will I be hit?
- Do I have to spar?
- What do I wear?
- What if I don't know the rules?
- Can I come alone?
- How do I choose the right class?

These are better content drivers than generic:

“Boxing improves your confidence.”


## 09.27 — PARENT VOICE

Parents need:


### FACTS BEFORE EMOTION.

Order:

1. age; 2. activity type; 3. contact rules;

4. supervision; 5. schedule; 6. location; 7. price; 8. developmental benefits.

Do not begin:

Offrez à votre enfant une aventure extraordinaire…

Start with:

La pratique dépend d'abord de l'âge de l'enfant et du format de cours proposé.


## 09.28 — PARENT SAFETY LANGUAGE

Avoid absolute:

100% sans risque.

Impossible.

Avoid:

Aucun danger.

Impossible.

Better:

Les formats éducatifs sont encadrés avec des règles adaptées à l'âge et au niveau. Les modalités exactes de contact sont précisées pour chaque programme.

Only say more when verified.


## 09.29 — CHILD DEVELOPMENT LANGUAGE

The brief wants:

- discipline;
- confidence;
- coordination;
- respect.

Good:

Les exercices travaillent notamment la coordination, l'écoute des consignes, le respect du partenaire et la confiance dans le mouvement.

Bad:

La boxe rend votre enfant plus discipliné.

That presents a guarantee.


## 09.30 — EXPERIENCED PRACTITIONER VOICE

Experienced users need less reassurance and more precision.

Good:

Ring, volumes de cours, coachs, créneaux et disciplines associées : voici les éléments à comparer.

Bad:

Que tu sois débutant ou expert, viens te dépasser dans une ambiance incroyable !

The experienced user doesn't need motivational fluff.


## 09.31 — MMA VOICE

Avoid fight-culture clichés.

No:

- warrior;
- cage fighter;
- ultimate;
- warrior mindset;
- combat total;
- brutal;
- warrior spirit.

Explain MMA technically and calmly.

The physical intensity of the discipline does not need amplification.


## 09.32 — FITNESS VOICE

Avoid:

- burn calories;
- beach body;
- shred;
- lose weight fast;
- transformation;
- summer body.

Unless a verified, responsibly framed programme explicitly owns those outcomes.

Focus on:

- cardio;
- movement;
- technique;
- conditioning;
- training enjoyment;
- non-competitive format.


## 09.33 — WOMEN'S PROGRAMME LANGUAGE

Never frame women's boxing as:

- easier;
- softer;
- safer because women;
- “boxing but feminine.”

Instead:

Un créneau entre femmes peut être une préférence de pratique. Des cours mixtes restent également accessibles selon le programme.

No pink-copy vocabulary.


## 09.34 — PRICING LANGUAGE

Prices require extreme clarity.

Always present:

PRICE + PERIOD + DURATION + ELIGIBILITY + WHAT IS INCLUDED + IMPORTANT CONDITION

Example:

**29 € / 4 semaines**

Then immediately:

Offre applicable selon les conditions actuellement publiées par Boxing Center.

Exact conditions from registry.


## 09.35 — NEVER ORPHAN THE PRICE

Bad:


### 29 €

Then 500px later:

engagement / conditions apply.

The relevant conditions stay near the amount.


## 09.36 — PRICE PROMOTION LANGUAGE

The brief explicitly prohibits aggressive promotional mechanics at launch:

- pop-ups;
- countdowns;
- aggressive banners;
- special-offer funnels.

Therefore:

Good:

Offre actuelle.

Bad:

DERNIÈRE CHANCE !!!

Bad:

Plus que quelques places.

unless there is genuine inventory.


## 09.37 — SCHEDULE LANGUAGE

Never:

Horaires Boxing Center Colomiers.

Instead:

Créneaux accessibles depuis Colomiers.

or:

Planning des clubs Boxing Center pertinents depuis Colomiers.


## 09.38 — TIME FRESHNESS

If information can change:

Mis à jour le 7 septembre 2026.

or:

Vérifié le 7 septembre 2026.

Use **verified** only if someone/process actually verified it.

Do not use freshness language as decoration.


## 09.39 — “ACTUELLEMENT”

This word becomes important.

For volatile claims:

Portet propose actuellement du MMA.

It communicates that programme truth can change.

Not every sentence needs it.

Use for:

- disciplines that might change;
- offers;
- schedule;
- coaches;
- price;
- facilities if renovation changes likely.


## 09.40 — CTA GRAMMAR

CTAs should tell the visitor exactly what happens.


### DISCOVERY

- Explorer la boxe anglaise
- Comprendre le MMA
- Voir les cours enfants


### DECISION

- Trouver mon club
- Comparer Minimes et Portet
- Voir où pratiquer le MMA


### PRACTICAL

- Voir les créneaux
- Consulter les tarifs
- Préparer ma première séance


### EXTERNAL

- Découvrir Boxing Center Portet ↗
- Voir le planning officiel de Minimes ↗


### HUMAN

- Poser une question
- Appeler Boxing Center


## 09.41 — CTA VERBS TO AVOID

Avoid generic:

- découvrir;
- en savoir plus;
- cliquer ici;
- commencer maintenant;
- rejoindre;
- profiter.

Not banned universally.

But a more precise verb is usually better.

“Découvrir Portet” is reasonable because the destination is explicit.

“Découvrir” alone is weak.


## 09.42 — PRIMARY CTA LIMIT

One clear primary action per local decision context.

Do not make three buttons all sound equally urgent.

Example:

Primary:

**TROUVER MON CLUB**

Secondary:

Voir les disciplines

Tertiary:

J'ai une question


## 09.43 — BUTTON COPY LENGTH

Prefer:

2–5 words.

Long descriptive external links may be longer when clarity demands it.

Example:

Voir le planning officiel de Minimes

is better than:

Planning

when leaving the Colomiers site.


## 09.44 — ANSWER-FIRST GEO GRAMMAR

Every important question should be answerable in a compact structure.

Template:

QUESTION DIRECT ANSWER. CRITICAL CONTEXT.

EVIDENCE / NEXT STEP.

Example:


### Y a-t-il un Boxing Center à Colomiers ?

Non. Boxing Center ne possède actuellement pas de salle physique à Colomiers. Ce site aide les habitants de Colomiers à choisir parmi les clubs du réseau adaptés à leur discipline, notamment Minimes et Portet-sur-Garonne.

That is enough.

Do not write 350 words before saying “non”.


## 09.45 — EXTRACTABLE ANSWER LENGTH

For many factual answers:

target roughly:

**40–120 words**

where that is enough to answer accurately.

Not as an SEO formula.

As a readability/extractability principle.

Complex questions can require longer answers.


## 09.46 — QUESTION HEADINGS SHOULD SOUND LIKE REAL QUESTIONS

Good:

Peut-on commencer le MMA sans expérience ?

Good:

Mon enfant va-t-il prendre des coups ?

Good:

Quel club choisir si je veux surtout faire de la boxe anglaise ?

Bad:

MMA COLMIERS DÉBUTANT FAQ

Never write for a parser at the expense of a human.


## 09.47 — QUERY TERMS

Google explicitly notes that its systems can understand many related search formulations without every exact variation appearing on a page.

Therefore:

we do **not** need:

club de boxe Colomiers, boxe club Colomiers, cours boxe Colomiers, boxe près Colomiers…

in the same paragraph.

Use language naturally.


## 09.48 — KEYWORD USAGE

Primary query language may naturally appear in:

- title;
- H1;
- opening answer;
- one or two useful subheadings;

- contextual internal links.

It should not be mathematically distributed.

No density target.


## 09.49 — NO SEO CONCLUSION BLOCK

Forbidden page ending:


### BOXE ANGLAISE COLOMIERS : VOTRE CLUB DE BOXE PROCHE DE COLOMIERS

followed by six keyword paragraphs.

If content is useful, integrate it into the journey.


## 09.50 — NO WORD-COUNT TARGET

Google explicitly says there is no preferred word count and warns against writing to a number because someone claims Search wants it.

Our metric:


### QUESTION COMPLETENESS

Does the visitor know enough to make the next decision?

If yes:

stop writing.


## 09.51 — PARAGRAPH LENGTH

Web reading default:

1–4 sentences per paragraph.

Longer only where argument truly benefits.

No endless text walls.

No artificial one-sentence-per-line staccato everywhere.


## 09.52 — BULLETS

Use bullets for genuinely parallel information:

- equipment;
- comparison criteria;
- documents;
- requirements.

Do not convert all prose into listicles.

The editorial system should still read like authored writing.


## 09.53 — EMPHASIS

Bold should identify meaning.

Examples:

**Il n'existe pas de salle physique Boxing Center à Colomiers.**

Good.

Do not bold half the paragraph for SEO.


## 09.54 — EXCLAMATION MARKS

Rare.

Default ceiling:

**zero or one per page**, excluding campaign content.

We don't need punctuation to manufacture energy.


## 09.55 — EMOJI

No emoji in core site copy.

The Boxing Center social voice may use them.

The website does not.


## 09.56 — SWEARING / SLANG

No.

The user's internal creative discussions may be high-energy.

Public site copy remains professional.


## 09.57 — SPORTS METAPHORS

Use sparingly.

Allowed when it adds meaning:

passer un cap.

Potentially:

premier round

but we have deliberately moved away from overusing literal boxing-round metaphors.

Avoid:

mettre K.-O. tes excuses.

monter sur le ring de la vie.

Absolutely not.


## 09.58 — BRAND NAME RULE

Canonical:

**Boxing Center**

Real locations:

**Boxing Center Toulouse Minimes**

**Boxing Center Portet-sur-Garonne**

or exact canonical public brand names from entity registry.

Do not arbitrarily shorten in metadata and expand elsewhere if that creates entity ambiguity.


## 09.59 — COLOMIERS CONTEXT LABEL

Preferred:

Boxing Center · Depuis Colomiers

Not a fake location brand.

This wording should remain consistent across:

- header;
- footer;

- OG;
- possibly breadcrumbs;
- machine documentation.


## 09.60 — SOURCE LANGUAGE

Where evidence matters:

```text
Source : Boxing Center Portet
```

or:

Programme vérifié auprès de Boxing Center Portet.

Only when that statement is actually true operationally.

Do not fake scholarly sourcing on every content block.


## 09.61 — PROVENANCE COPY

Image caption:

Boxing Center Minimes · Toulouse

Facility caption:

Cage MMA · Boxing Center Portet

This is preferable to:

Notre cage MMA.

because the location matters.


## 09.62 — REVIEWS LANGUAGE

Never rewrite a review in a way that changes meaning.

If quoting:

- exact or accurately excerpted;
- source;
- real physical club;
- consent/platform policy respected.

Never synthesize:

“Les adhérents adorent…”

from several reviews.


## 09.63 — REVIEW ATTRIBUTION

Good:

Avis Google · Boxing Center Minimes

Not:

Avis Boxing Center Colomiers.

There is no such physical entity.


## 09.64 — FORM LABEL LANGUAGE

Use plain French:

- Prénom
- Nom
- Téléphone
- E-mail
- Activité recherchée
- Club souhaité
- Message

Not:

Identité utilisateur

Coordonnées de contact

Objet de votre demande

unless formality is required.


## 09.65 — FORM HELP COPY

Good:

Tu ne sais pas quel club choisir ? Sélectionne “Recommandez-moi”.

Good:

Un numéro ou une adresse e-mail suffit pour qu'on puisse te répondre.

Only if the form truly behaves that way.


## 09.66 — FORM ERROR VOICE

Errors are:


### SPECIFIC + CALM + ACTIONABLE.

Bad:

Champ invalide.

Good:

Saisis une adresse e-mail complète, par exemple nom@domaine.fr.

Bad:

ERROR.

Good:

Il manque un moyen de te contacter.


## 09.67 — ERROR MUST NOT BLAME

Avoid:

Vous avez mal renseigné votre email.

Better:

Cette adresse e-mail semble incomplète.


## 09.68 — EMPTY STATE VOICE

Empty state helps.

Bad:

Aucun résultat.

Better:

Aucun créneau vérifié ne correspond à ce choix pour le moment. Essaie un autre jour ou consulte le planning officiel du club.


## 09.69 — NO-MATCH RECOMMENDATION

Good:

On n'a pas de correspondance exacte avec ces critères.

Then:

Si tu gardes le MMA mais modifies l'horaire, Portet redevient une option.

This turns failure into decision support.


## 09.70 — LOADING COPY

Only when something genuinely loads.

Good:

Vérification des créneaux…

Not:

Nous recherchons ton club idéal…

if the deterministic answer exists instantly.

No fake AI theatre.


## 09.71 — SUCCESS COPY

Good:


### ON A CE QU'IL FAUT.

Ta demande concerne le MMA et tu souhaites être orienté vers le club adapté. L'équipe pourra te répondre avec ce contexte.

Then next actions.


## 09.72 — 404 VOICE

Locked territory:


### MAUVAISE DIRECTION. PAS MAUVAIS DÉPART.

Supporting:

Cette page n'existe pas. Repars depuis les disciplines ou trouve directement le club qui te correspond.

Characterful.

Still useful.


## 09.73 — PRIVACY COPY

Privacy copy must be explicit and boring in the best sense.

Explain:

- what is collected;
- why;
- who receives it;
- retention basis/duration where applicable;
- user rights;
- contact.

No:

Nous respectons votre vie privée ❤️

instead of actual information.


## 09.74 — IMAGE ALT SYSTEM

The client brief requests descriptive ALT text.

Our rule is stronger:

**ALT describes the image's purpose in its page context, not its SEO target.**

W3C's current guidance distinguishes informative, decorative, functional and complex images, and recommends empty `alt=""` for purely decorative or redundant images rather than forcing descriptions onto everything.


## 09.75 — INFORMATIVE IMAGE ALT

Example:

Image:

people training on heavy bags at Minimes.

Good:

Des pratiquants travaillent sur les sacs de frappe au Boxing Center Minimes à Toulouse.

Potentially bad:

Salle de boxe Boxing Center avec sacs.

Too vague if location matters.

Terrible:

club boxe Colomiers MMA Colomiers sport de combat Colomiers.


## 09.76 — DECORATIVE IMAGE ALT

If the same Minimes photo is used merely as atmospheric background and the location/information already appears nearby:

```text
alt=""
```

W3C explicitly recommends a null alternative for decorative images so assistive technology can ignore them.


## 09.77 — FUNCTIONAL IMAGE ALT

If an image is the only content in a linked control:

alt communicates **destination/function**, not visual appearance. W3C's current image guidance makes this distinction explicit.

But our design should rarely use image-only navigation.


## 09.78 — COMPLEX GRAPHICS

Route visualizations/data diagrams should have their equivalent meaning in normal text.

Do not attempt to write a 400-word alt attribute describing the graph.

W3C recommends providing the full equivalent information elsewhere for complex imagery rather than relying on an oversized alt description.


## 09.79 — ALT LENGTH

Short enough to communicate purpose.

W3C's current guidance recommends concise text alternatives and moving substantial explanation into surrounding content when necessary.

No hard character limit.


## 09.80 — IMAGE FILENAME

The brief suggests descriptive filenames such as:

`club-boxe-colomiers-boxing-center.jpg`.

We need to correct the geographic implication where the actual photo is of Minimes.

Better:

boxing-center-minimes-sacs-boxe.webp boxing-center-portet-cage-mma.webp cours-boxe-enfants-minimes.webp

The file itself should tell the truth.

The page context supplies Colomiers relevance.


## 09.81 — CAPTIONS VS ALT

Caption:

visible editorial context.

Alt:

non-visual equivalent.

They may overlap but should not mechanically duplicate each other.

Example:

Caption:

Boxing Center Minimes · Toulouse

Alt:

Une coach encadre un groupe pendant un exercice de boxe anglaise au Boxing Center Minimes.

Excellent.


## 09.82 — META TITLE VOICE

Metadata titles should be more utilitarian than display headings.

Formula generally:

PRIMARY INTENT + LOCAL RELATIONSHIP | BRAND

Example:

MMA près de Colomiers | Où s'entraîner avec Boxing Center

Not:

MMA COLOMIERS | CLUB MMA COLOMIERS | BOXING CENTER


## 09.83 — META DESCRIPTION VOICE

Description must:

- answer context;
- name useful differentiator;
- invite useful action;
- avoid false location implications.

Not keyword lists.


## 09.84 — OG COPY

OG can be more editorial.

Example:


### MMA


### DEPUIS COLOMIERS

Then route visual.

But context must remain truthful.

Do not render:

BOXING CENTER COLOMIERS — MMA

as though it were a club location.


## 09.85 — CONTENT SOURCE HIERARCHY

When writing factual content, authority order:

1. CLAIM REGISTRY 2. VERIFIED LOCATION DATA 3. OFFICIAL NETWORK DATA 4. AUTHORITATIVE EXTERNAL SOURCE 5. EDITORIAL INTERPRETATION

Search-result snippets or memory are not canonical facts.


## 09.86 — CONTENT CREATION WORKFLOW

Every major factual content block follows:

QUESTION ↓ REQUIRED CLAIMS ↓ VERIFY CLAIMS ↓ WRITE ANSWER ↓ CHECK GEOGRAPHIC LANGUAGE ↓ CHECK USER VALUE

↓ CHECK INTERNAL LINKS ↓ COPY EDIT ↓ FACT RECHECK

Not:

write 1,500 words about MMA and fact-check later.


## 09.87 — AI-ASSISTED CONTENT POLICY

AI is allowed to help:

- structure;
- research planning;
- wording alternatives;
- compression;
- consistency checking;
- metadata drafting;
- duplicate detection;
- terminology checking.

It is **not** allowed to become the factual source.

Google's current generative-AI guidance explicitly permits useful AI-assisted content but emphasizes accuracy, quality and relevance, and warns that generating many pages without added value can violate scaled-content spam policies.


## 09.88 — AI CONTENT RULE


### AI MAY TRANSFORM TRUTH. IT MAY NOT INVENT TRUTH.

Input:

verified facts.

Output:

high-quality editorial expression.

Never:

prompt model:

write a detailed page about Boxing Center Portet.

and accept whatever details appear.


## 09.89 — AI PAGE FACTORY IS FORBIDDEN

No generating:

boxe-tournefeuille boxe-blagnac boxe-pibrac boxe-cugnaux ...

because a keyword spreadsheet exists.

Google's spam policy specifically identifies large-scale low-value generated pages as scaled content abuse regardless of whether they are created by AI, human automation or other means.

This reinforces Part 02's route discipline.


## 09.90 — CONTENT MUST ADD SOMETHING

Google's people-first guidance asks whether content provides original information, substantial value compared with other search results, and enough information to help someone accomplish their goal.

Our internal equivalent:

Every page must contain at least one of:

- Boxing Center-specific evidence;
- actual comparison;
- real first-session information;
- verified schedule;
- verified pricing;
- useful decision mechanism;
- transparent location relationship.

Generic discipline education alone is insufficient.


## 09.91 — CONTENT SHOULD NOT PRETEND TO BE FRESH

Never change:

Mis à jour le 7 septembre 2026

to:

Mis à jour le 10 septembre 2026

because the build happened again.

Freshness date changes only when the relevant content/data was materially reverified or changed.

Google explicitly warns against changing dates purely to make pages appear fresh.


## 09.92 — AUTHORSHIP

Not every commercial landing page requires a giant author profile.

But ownership should be clear.

Potential footer/content metadata:

Informations Boxing Center · données clubs vérifiées par l'équipe Boxing Center.

Only if operationally true.

Where specialist educational content becomes substantial, named reviewer/coach attribution can strengthen trust.

Do not invent bylines.


## 09.93 — “WHO / HOW / WHY”

Google currently recommends thinking explicitly about:

- who created the content;
- how it was created;
- why it exists;

as part of trustworthy, people-first publishing.

For us:


### WHO

Boxing Center / responsible editorial owner.


### HOW

Verified network/location facts + editorial processing.


### WHY

Help Colomiers residents choose appropriately.

Not:

rank for Colomiers.


## 09.94 — CONTENT REVIEW OWNERSHIP

Critical discipline/process content should have an internal reviewer.

Potential:

Editorial owner Technical/SEO reviewer Club factual owner Coach/sport reviewer where needed

Not every comma requires committee approval.

But high-risk facts do.


## 09.95 — FACTS REQUIRING HUMAN VERIFICATION

Especially:

- age limits;
- contact/sparring rules;
- coach qualifications;
- opening hours;
- schedules;
- prices;
- offer eligibility;
- equipment availability;
- trial-session details;
- federation affiliation;
- club addresses;
- phone/email;
- medical/safety statements.

No model inference.


## 09.96 — MEDICAL CLAIMS

Avoid.

Do not claim boxing:

- treats anxiety;
- cures depression;
- fixes ADHD;
- guarantees weight loss;
- improves cardiovascular disease;
- prevents bullying.

We may discuss ordinary fitness/training benefits cautiously where appropriate.

This is a sports site, not a medical publisher.


## 09.97 — SAFETY CLAIMS

Avoid absolutes:

- safe;
- risk-free;
- no injuries;
- impossible to get hurt.

Use specific procedural facts instead.


## 09.98 — COMPETITOR LANGUAGE

We do not attack competitors.

No:

Unlike other clubs in Colomiers…

unless a specific, factual comparison has genuine consumer value and is verified.

The site's differentiation comes from Boxing Center's offer.

Not competitor criticism.


## 09.99 — BACKLINK COPY

The brief explicitly requires links toward Minimes and Portet.

Anchors should be contextual:

Découvrir Boxing Center Portet-sur-Garonne

Consulter le planning officiel de Boxing Center Minimes

Not:

cliquez ici

and not stuffed:

club de boxe Toulouse Minimes pas cher meilleur club.


## 09.100 — INTERNAL LINK COPY

Link based on the **next question**.

Example after MMA explanation:

Voir quel club du réseau propose actuellement le MMA.

After beginner section:

Préparer ta première séance.

After children's age section:

Voir les créneaux enfants.

This creates reasoning continuity.


## 09.101 — DUPLICATION RULE

A canonical fact may appear on multiple pages.

A canonical **explanation** should not be cloned everywhere.

Example truth:

no physical Colomiers club.

May recur.

But exact same 180-word block should not be pasted on nine pages.

Each page states the fact in its own relevant context.


## 09.102 — CONTENT OVERLAP HEURISTIC

Our internal heuristic:

If two pages share more than roughly 60% of their meaningful narrative blocks:

investigate.

Either:

- merge;
- differentiate;
- reduce repetition.

This is an internal quality test.

Not a Google rule.


## 09.103 — FAQ SYSTEM

FAQ questions come from:

- real user uncertainty;
- Search Console queries after launch;
- sales/contact questions;
- coach/front-desk knowledge;
- common beginner/parent concerns.

Not generated from “People Also Ask” alone.


## 09.104 — FAQ ANSWERS

Short answer first.

Then depth.

Example:


### Faut-il acheter des gants avant la première séance ?

Pas nécessairement. Cela dépend du club et du format de séance. Vérifie l'information indiquée pour ton club avant de venir.

If source says equipment is loaned:

say so.

Do not generalize from one location automatically.


## 09.105 — CONTENT TENSION

We want copy that can be visually strong without becoming empty.

Example:


### LE MMA NE COMMENCE PAS PAR UN COMBAT.

Then useful explanation.

Not:


### DÉPASSE TES LIMITES.

followed by nothing.

Every strong line must earn its drama with information.


## 09.106 — SHORT COPY TEST

Remove the photography.

Does the headline still communicate something meaningful?

If no:

rewrite.


## 09.107 — LONG COPY TEST

Remove the headline.

Does the body still contain concrete useful information?

If no:

rewrite.


## 09.108 — SEO TEST

Remove “Colomiers” from every unnecessary repeated sentence.

Does the page still clearly serve Colomiers because:

- title;
- H1;
- opening;
- destination logic;
- local context

remain clear?

If yes:

good.

If the page loses all geographic relevance without keyword repetition:

the content architecture is weak.


## 09.109 — GEO TEST

Give an AI only the page text.

Can it answer correctly:

- Is there a physical Boxing Center in Colomiers?
- Which clubs are relevant?
- What discipline is discussed?
- Who is it for?
- What should they do next?

If not:

rewrite.


## 09.110 — BEGINNER TEST

Ask:

Does this content make the user feel stupid for not knowing something?

If yes:

rewrite.


## 09.111 — EXPERIENCED TEST

Ask:

Does this sound like the writer has never entered a boxing gym?

If yes:

rewrite.


## 09.112 — PARENT TEST

Ask:

Did we answer safety/contact/age before trying to inspire them?

If no:

reorder.


## 09.113 — COPY HATER TEST

For every paragraph:


### Could any gym publish this?

If yes:

make it more specific or delete.


### Is this adjective doing the work evidence should do?

If yes:

replace.


### Is this sentence only here for SEO?

If yes:

delete/rewrite.


### Is the geographical wording completely truthful?

If no:

reject.


### Is certainty greater than evidence?

If yes:

qualify.


### Does the reader know what to do next?

If relevant and no:

improve.


### Does it sound generated?

If yes:

rewrite manually.


## 09.114 — “AI SMELL” DETECTOR

Phrases likely to trigger rewriting:

- que vous soyez débutant ou confirmé;
- bien plus qu'un sport;
- dépassez vos limites;
- repoussez vos limites;
- une expérience unique;
- dans une ambiance conviviale;
- passionnés et expérimentés;
- pour tous les niveaux;
- discipline complète;
- cadre idéal;
- votre allié;
- au cœur de;
- une approche personnalisée;
- une communauté dynamique;
- atteindre vos objectifs;
- révélez votre potentiel.

Some may occasionally be factually usable.

But the combination is classic generic web copy.

Default:

rewrite.


## 09.115 — BEFORE / AFTER EXAMPLES


### GENERIC

Que vous soyez débutant ou confirmé, nos coachs passionnés vous accompagnent dans une ambiance conviviale pour vous permettre d'atteindre vos objectifs.


### BAFFLED

Tu débutes ? Le premier cours sert d'abord à comprendre les bases. Tu pratiques déjà ? Compare les créneaux, les coachs et les équipements du club qui correspond à ta discipline.

Specific.

Useful.


## 09.116 — GENERIC

Découvrez le MMA, une discipline complète qui combine plusieurs techniques.


### BAFFLED

En MMA, tu dois apprendre à gérer trois distances : debout, au corps à corps et au sol. Si l'une de ces dimensions t'intéresse davantage, le grappling ou la boxe peuvent aussi être une meilleure porte d'entrée.

Information.

Decision.


## 09.117 — GENERIC

La boxe pour enfants développe confiance, discipline et respect.


### BAFFLED

Avant de choisir un cours, regarde d'abord l'âge, le niveau de contact et le format proposé. Les séances éducatives peuvent ensuite travailler la coordination, l'écoute des consignes, le respect du partenaire et la confiance dans le mouvement.

Responsible.


## 09.118 — GENERIC

Profitez d'horaires flexibles adaptés à votre emploi du temps.


### BAFFLED

Tu peux venir surtout le soir ? Filtre les créneaux du réseau et regarde quel club propose réellement ta discipline à ce moment-là.

Product copy.

Not marketing.


## 09.119 — CONTENT STRUCTURE IN MARKDOWN

Source content files should use clear semantic structure.

Example:

--- title: description: primaryIntent: reviewedAt: claimDependencies: --- # H1 Opening answer. ## Section Content. ### Question Direct answer.

No rich text blobs with arbitrary styling data embedded everywhere.


## 09.120 — CONTENT VS DATA

Separate:


### Editorial content

explanation.


### Structured business data

prices, times, addresses, clubs.

Do not mix volatile values deep into Markdown if they can be referenced programmatically.

Example:

Bad:

Le club est ouvert jusqu'à 21h30.

Better:

Les horaires d'ouverture sont affichés ci-dessous.

Then `<OpeningHours clubId="portet" />`.


## 09.121 — CONTENT TOKENS

Potential editorial placeholders/components:

<ClubName id="portet" /> <VerifiedPrice offerId="..." /> <OpeningHours clubId="..." /> <ScheduleLink clubId="..." />

Exact Astro/content architecture later.

Purpose:

prevent stale embedded data.


## 09.122 — CONTENT REVIEW FLAGS

Draft content may contain internal flags:

[VERIFY] [SOURCE NEEDED] [CONFLICT] [LEGAL REVIEW] [CLUB CONFIRMATION]

Production build must fail if unresolved critical flags remain.


## 09.123 — SPELLING / FRENCH QA

Release requires automated + human checks for:

- spelling;
- accents;
- punctuation;
- apostrophes;
- typography;
- capitalization;
- club names;
- discipline names;
- URLs;
- contact details.

We already found public-site typos in prior work.

That cannot recur here.


## 09.124 — FRENCH TYPOGRAPHY

Use French conventions consistently:

- non-breaking space before €;

- appropriate spacing before : ; ? !;

- apostrophe ’ where production typography supports it;

- 1er, not 1ier;

- 4 semaines;

- 12 mois;

- 21h15, not random 21:15 in French editorial copy;

- Portet-sur-Garonne;

- États-Unis;

- St-Cyprien / Saint-Cyprien according to canonical entity form.

Implementation should preserve non-breaking units.


## 09.125 — PHONE FORMATTING

Visible French phone:

05 62 24 46 82

Link:

```text
tel:+33562244682
```

assuming number verification succeeds.

Do not expose inconsistent formatting across pages.


## 09.126 — EMAIL

Visible exactly as authoritative registry determines.

`mailto:` link.

No image-of-email trick.

Spam prevention must not reduce accessibility.


## 09.127 — DATES

Visible French:

7 septembre 2026

Compact metadata may use:

07.09.2026

but we should choose a consistent display convention by component.

Machine:

ISO date.


## 09.128 — CONTENT VERSIONING

Meaningful factual edits should be traceable in Git.

No need to expose revision history publicly.

But internally:

we should know:

- what changed;
- why;
- source.

Particularly for:

schedule prices club facts contact.


## 09.129 — CONTENT EXPIRY

Volatile claims may include:

reviewAfter

Example:

schedule → short expiry offer → expiration date opening hours → periodic review address → low frequency discipline description → no automatic expiry

This turns content maintenance into a system.


## 09.130 — STALE CONTENT BEHAVIOR

If a volatile claim exceeds its review window:

it should trigger internal QA.

Depending severity:

- warning;
- build failure;
- hide/qualify.

Do not silently publish stale claims forever.


## 09.131 — CONTENT MACHINE LAYER

Machine documents such as:

llms.txt

must be generated from the same facts/content graph.

They do not receive separate copywriting.

The visible website is canonical.

Part 10 will formalize this.


## 09.132 — CONTENT + AI SEARCH

Google's 2026 guidance for AI experiences continues to say that the same foundational Search practices apply, with particular value placed on useful, unique, non-commodity content; there is no separate magical content format required to appear in AI features.

Therefore:

we do not write “for AI.”

**We write clear factual content whose structure is easy for humans and machines to understand.**


## 09.133 — CONTENT “+1”

The editorial +1 is:


### THE COPY ADMITS WHAT IT DOESN'T KNOW.

Most marketing sites only publish positive certainties.

Our content system can say:

Aucun créneau vérifié ne correspond actuellement à ce choix.

Cette information doit encore être confirmée.

Les horaires peuvent varier : voici la source actuelle.

Boxing Center ne possède pas de salle physique à Colomiers.

This is not weakness.

It creates **epistemic character**.

The website sounds intelligent because it knows the boundary between:

fact inference unknown.


## 09.134 — SECOND EDITORIAL +1


### QUESTIONS GET SHARPER AS THE USER MOVES.

Homepage:

Qu'est-ce que tu cherches ?

MMA page:

Tu veux surtout travailler debout ou aussi au sol ?

Club engine:

Quand peux-tu venir ?

Planning:

Quel créneau est réellement compatible ?

Final:

Tu sais où aller. Quand commences-tu ?

The editorial voice itself follows **The Resolving Route**.

Language narrows with the decision.


## 09.135 — CONTENT INTENSITY CURVE

HOME broad / inviting DISCIPLINE educational / precise DECISION comparative / factual PLANNING functional FIRST SESSION human / reassuring FINAL short / decisive

Like motion, copy becomes calmer and more certain.


## 09.136 — CONTENT ACCEPTANCE GATE

No page ships until:


### TRUTH

Every factual statement is supportable.


### LOCATION

No accidental Colomiers physical-location claim.


### VOICE

Direct, calm, expert.


### ORIGINALITY

Not generic sports copy.


### BEGINNER ACCESS

Technical terms explained.


### EXPERIENCED CREDIBILITY

No childish simplification.


### PARENT RESPONSIBILITY

No safety/development guarantees.


### SEO

Search language natural.


### GEO

Critical answers are directly extractable.


### CLAIM CONTROL

Volatile values come from registry.


### ACCESSIBILITY

Alt/captions/forms are purposeful.


### CONVERSION

CTA states what happens.


### FRENCH

Typography/spelling professionally reviewed.


### AI

No unsupported model-generated facts.


### BAFFLED

Could this copy have been written for any gym?

If yes:

rewrite.


## 09.137 — LOCKED EDITORIAL RULES

We now lock:


### TUTOIEMENT, NOT TEENAGE SLANG

Direct without being juvenile.


### FACTS BEFORE ADJECTIVES

Evidence does the selling.


### CERTAINTY IS EARNED

Language never exceeds evidence.


### NO FAKE LOCALISM

Colomiers is the user's origin, not a fake branch.


### ANSWER FIRST

Never hide the direct answer beneath SEO prose.


### QUESTIONS GET SHARPER

Language narrows as the journey resolves.


### AI TRANSFORMS TRUTH — IT DOES NOT CREATE TRUTH

Structured facts remain authoritative.


### NO KEYWORD WALLS

Search relevance comes from meaning.


### NO GENERIC GYM VOICE

Every paragraph must solve something.


### THE SITE MAY SAY “WE DON'T KNOW YET”

Honest uncertainty is part of the brand.


## 09.138 — CONTENT SYSTEM IN ONE SENTENCE

**Boxing Center Colomiers speaks like an experienced coach who understands** **both the sport and the visitor: direct enough to answer immediately, precise** **enough not to exaggerate, calm enough to reassure, and honest enough to** **say exactly where the limits of the information are.**


<a id="part-10-seo-geo-entity-metadata-system"></a>

# PART 10 — SEO, GEO, Entity & Metadata System


## 10.0 — PURPOSE

The client brief asks every page to have:

- an optimized title;
- a meta description;
- one H1;
- structured H2s;
- local keyword relevance;
- internal linking;
- backlinks toward the targeted Boxing Center sites;
- optimized images;
- descriptive ALT;
- clean URLs;
- high loading speed;
- strong mobile performance.

Those are minimum requirements.

Part 10 turns them into an integrated **machine-understanding architecture**.

The objective is not merely:

rank the website.

It is:

**make every machine that encounters Boxing Center Colomiers understand** **exactly what the site is, what it is not, what every page is about, which** **physical entities actually exist, where each fact originates, which URL owns** **each concept, and which pieces of information may be trusted.**

This includes:

- Google;
- Bing;
- Copilot;
- ChatGPT Search;
- other answer engines;
- browser link previews;
- social platforms;
- traditional crawlers;
- future agents.

The architecture must never require an AI system to infer:

“Maybe there is a Boxing Center gym in Colomiers.”

The answer must be machine-obvious:


### THERE IS A COLOMIERS INFORMATION/ORIENTATION PROPERTY. THERE IS NOT A COLOMIERS PHYSICAL BOXING CENTER LOCATION.

That distinction governs the entire layer.


## 10.1 — THE MACHINE TRUTH DOCTRINE

The single most important rule is:


### ONE FACT → ONE AUTHORITY → MANY REPRESENTATIONS

Never:

HTML truth ≠ JSON-LD truth ≠ llms.txt truth ≠ metadata truth ≠ assistant truth

Instead:

CLAIM REGISTRY ↓ ENTITY GRAPH

↓ PAGE CONTENT ↓ HTML JSON-LD METADATA OPEN GRAPH LLMS FILES INTERNAL TOOLS

Every representation is derived from the same source.


## 10.2 — SEO AND GEO ARE NOT TWO SEPARATE WEBSITES

Google's current 2026 guidance says its generative AI search features remain grounded in the same core search systems, and that normal SEO fundamentals continue to apply. Google explicitly says there are **no special additional requirements** for appearing in AI Overviews or AI Mode.

Bing's current Webmaster Guidelines make essentially the same point for Bing/Copilot grounding: crawlability, indexing accuracy, URL consolidation, content clarity and authority also support AI-grounding eligibility.

Therefore:


### GEO DOES NOT REPLACE SEO. GEO DEEPENS INFORMATION QUALITY.

Our GEO layer concentrates on:

- entity clarity;
- direct answers;
- provenance;
- factual consistency;
- semantic relationships;
- stable URLs;

- extractability;
- freshness;
- evidence.

Not magical “AI keywords.”


## 10.3 — THE ENTITY GRAPH

The conceptual graph is:

```text
BOXING CENTER
Organization
│
├── BOXING CENTER MINIMES
│ Real physical sports location
│ Toulouse
│
├── BOXING CENTER PORTET
│ Real physical sports location
│ Portet-sur-Garonne
│
└── BOXINGCENTER-COLOMIERS.FR
WebSite
│
├── spatial/content context → Colomiers
├── boxe anglaise
├── MMA
├── boxing fitness
├── enfants
├── first-session guidance
├── planning orientation
└── comparison/orientation
```

There is intentionally **no node**:

BOXING CENTER COLOMIERS LocalBusiness Colomiers address

because that physical entity does not exist.


## 10.4 — COLOMIERS IS A CONTENT LOCATION, NOT A BUSINESS LOCATION

Schema.org provides `spatialCoverage` to describe the place that a creative work focuses on. That is a much better semantic model for this website than manufacturing a business address: the pages can be **about or geographically focused on Colomiers** without saying the organisation is physically located there.

Therefore a `WebPage` may legitimately carry:

"spatialCoverage": { "@type": "Place", "name": "Colomiers" }

This says:

the content concerns Colomiers.

It does **not** say:

the business is located in Colomiers.

That distinction is ideal for this project.


## 10.5 — COLOMIERS MUST NEVER RECEIVE LocalBusiness

Google recommends using `LocalBusiness` when marking up an actual physical business/location and recommends choosing the most specific local-business subtype appropriate for that business.

Schema.org itself defines `LocalBusiness` as a **particular physical business or branch**.

Therefore:

{ "@type": "LocalBusiness", "name": "Boxing Center Colomiers" }

is forbidden.

Likewise:

{ "@type": "ExerciseGym", "name": "Boxing Center Colomiers" }

Forbidden.

And:

{ "@type": "SportsClub", "address": { "addressLocality": "Colomiers" } }

Forbidden.

No markup hack may contradict physical reality.


## 10.6 — REAL CLUB ENTITY TYPES

Schema.org currently places:

- SportsClub;

- ExerciseGym;

- HealthClub;

under `SportsActivityLocation`, itself a subtype of `LocalBusiness`.

For Minimes and Portet, the exact subtype should be selected according to their **actual** **legal/operational identity**, not according to which markup looks most powerful.

Possible:

SportsClub

if the entity is most accurately a sports club.

Or:

ExerciseGym

if the location operates primarily as a gym/training facility.

We do **not** choose both blindly.

And we do not choose a type merely because Google supports Local Business rich results.


## 10.7 — THE MAIN ORGANIZATION ENTITY

The parent brand/entity is:


### Boxing Center

Not:

`Boxing Center Colomiers`.

The Organization record may include, once verified:

name logo official URL telephone if truly network-level email if truly network-level sameAs official social profiles legal identifiers where appropriate

Google recommends Organization structured data on a homepage or organizational page and says publishers should include relevant real-world and online administrative details while using the most specific appropriate subtype.

No property is added merely to make the graph larger.


## 10.8 — STABLE ENTITY IDs

Every important entity receives a persistent JSON-LD `@id`.

Conceptually:

https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/#website https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/#organization https://boxe-toulouse.com/#club https://boxing-center-portet.fr/#club https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/#colomiers

Exact external `@id`s should align with the canonical structured-data identity used on those destination sites where possible.

Do not change entity IDs when redesigning.

IDs identify entities.

Not page layouts.


## 10.9 — HOME JSON-LD GRAPH

Conceptual blueprint:

{ "@context": "https://schema.org", "@graph": [

{ "@type": "WebSite", "@id": "https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/#website", "url": "https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/", "name": "Boxing Center depuis Colomiers", "publisher": { "@id": "https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/#organization" }, "inLanguage": "fr-FR" }, { "@type": "Organization", "@id": "https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/#organization", "name": "Boxing Center", "logo": { "@type": "ImageObject", "url": "[VERIFIED OFFICIAL LOGO URL]" }, "sameAs": [ "[VERIFIED OFFICIAL PROFILE]" ] }, { "@type": "WebPage", "@id": "https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/#webpage", "url": "https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/", "name": "Boxe, MMA & sports de combat près de Colomiers", "isPartOf": { "@id": "https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/#website" }, "about": { "@id": "https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/#organization" }, "spatialCoverage": { "@id": "https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/#colomiers" }, "inLanguage": "fr-FR" }, { "@type": "Place", "@id": "https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/#colomiers", "name": "Colomiers" } ]

```text
}
```

This is illustrative architecture.

The implementation must generate values from the entity registry.


## 10.10 — REAL CLUBS INSIDE THE GRAPH

When a page materially describes Minimes or Portet, the graph may reference the verified physical entity.

Example concept:

{ "@type": "SportsClub", "@id": "https://boxe-toulouse.com/#club", "name": "Boxing Center Toulouse Minimes", "url": "https://boxe-toulouse.com/", "address": { "@type": "PostalAddress", "streetAddress": "[VERIFIED]", "addressLocality": "Toulouse", "postalCode": "[VERIFIED]", "addressCountry": "FR" } }

But only if those same details are:

- verified;
- visible or contextually supported on the page;
- consistent with the destination site.

No structured-data-only facts.


## 10.11 — DO NOT MAKE THE CLUB THE mainEntity OF EVERY PAGE

For:

```text
/mma/
```

the main subject is the MMA-from-Colomiers decision/content page.

Portet may be:

mentions

or a related entity.

For:

```text
/quel-club/
```

both physical clubs are relevant entities.

Do not make Portet `mainEntity` merely because the recommendation currently favors it.

The route itself remains a Colomiers orientation page.


## 10.12 — RELATIONSHIPS ARE NOT sameAs

This is important.

`sameAs` means:

another URL represents this same entity/identity.

It does **not** mean:

related site.

Therefore:

Minimes is not `sameAs` Portet.

Portet is not `sameAs` Colomiers.

The Boutique is not automatically `sameAs` a gym.

Sister sites use actual semantic relationships or normal links.

Only genuine identity-equivalent official profiles belong in `sameAs`.


## 10.13 — branchOf

Schema.org provides `branchOf` on local-business entities.

If—and only if—the legal/brand structure confirms Minimes and Portet are branches of the same Boxing Center organisation, they may reference:

"branchOf": { "@id": ".../#organization" }

If the contractual/legal entity relationship differs:

do not infer it from shared branding.

Claim Registry first.


## 10.14 — MARKUP SHOULD BE MINIMAL AND TRUE

We explicitly reject:


### SCHEMA MAXIMALISM

No dumping:

Organization LocalBusiness Product Service Course FAQPage HowTo Person Event AggregateRating Review Offer ItemList VideoObject ImageObject Action

onto every page because a plugin can.

Every schema type must answer:

What real entity or content object does this describe?

If there is no clear answer:

remove it.


## 10.15 — FAQ STRUCTURED DATA

Important current change:

Google deprecated FAQ rich results on **May 7, 2026**.

Therefore:

we still create high-quality FAQs for:

- visitors;
- content completeness;
- answer engines;

- accessibility;
- long-tail search;

but we do **not** build the project around FAQ rich-result markup.

Default:

no `FAQPage` JSON-LD purely for Google visibility.

Visible Q&A content matters more.


## 10.16 — FIRST-SESSION PAGE IS NOT HowTo BY DEFAULT

`/premiere-seance/` describes:

what will happen.

It is not necessarily a formal instructional task.

Do not force `HowTo` schema simply because the page has numbered steps.

Semantic type:

`WebPage`.

Simple.

Accurate.


## 10.17 — CONTACT PAGE

`/contact/` can use:

ContactPage

as the WebPage subtype.

Its visible contact information must match the contact registry.

No Colomiers address.


## 10.18 — PRICING SCHEMA

Membership/subscription pricing may semantically use Schema.org `Offer` objects where useful.

But we do not automatically mark gym memberships as Google `Product` rich-result objects.

Only implement richer commercial markup where:

- the offering semantically qualifies;
- the offer is visibly shown;
- conditions match;
- Google's current supported feature guidelines genuinely apply.

No markup theatre.


## 10.19 — REVIEWS / RATINGS SCHEMA

Default on Colomiers:


### NO AggregateRating MARKUP

Reasons:

- there is no Colomiers physical listing;
- reviews belong to real club entities;
- misattribution could imply a fake local reputation.

If reviews are displayed:

identify the real club.

Example:

Avis Google · Boxing Center Minimes.

Do not synthesize a network/Colomiers star score.


## 10.20 — BREADCRUMBS

Use visible breadcrumbs and `BreadcrumbList` structured data on inner routes.

Google says breadcrumbs may help users understand a page's position in the site hierarchy and recommends representing a typical navigation path rather than blindly mirroring the raw URL.

Examples:

Accueil → MMA Accueil → Boxe enfants Accueil → Première séance

Simple.

No fake:

Accueil → Colomiers → Sports → Combat → MMA

merely to create keyword depth.


## 10.21 — THE CANONICAL HOST

The formal specification names:


### www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr

Therefore the recommended canonical origin is:

https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr

Lock this unless infrastructure gives a compelling reason to revise before implementation.

Then:

http://boxingcenter-colomiers.fr http://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr https://boxingcenter-colomiers.fr

permanently redirect to:

https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr


## 10.22 — HTTPS ONLY

Every canonical URL:

`https`.

HTTP exists only to redirect.

No mixed-content assets.

No canonicals pointing HTTP while browser redirects HTTPS.


## 10.23 — TRAILING-SLASH POLICY

Recommended:

/ /mma/ /boxe-anglaise/

Use one convention everywhere.

Astro configuration, internal links, canonicals, sitemap and redirects all agree.

No simultaneous:

/mma /mma/

200 responses.

One is canonical.

The other redirects.


## 10.24 — CANONICAL URL MATRIX

/ self-canonical /boxe-anglaise/ self-canonical /mma/ self-canonical /boxing-fitness/ self-canonical /boxe-enfants/ self-canonical /premiere-seance/ self-canonical /quel-club/ self-canonical /plannings/ self-canonical /tarifs/ self-canonical /contact/ self-canonical

Legal routes receive their own policy.


## 10.25 — QUERY PARAMETERS

Example:

```text
/quel-club/?discipline=mma&niveau=debutant
```

This is an interaction state.

Not a new search page.

Canonical:

https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/quel-club/

Likewise:

```text
/plannings/?jour=mercredi
```

canonical to:

```text
/plannings/
```

Google currently identifies sorting/filtering parameters as a common source of duplicate URLs and recommends clear canonical signals.


## 10.26 — DO NOT INDEX PARAMETER COMBINATIONS

We do not generate crawlable internal anchors for thousands of filter states.

Controls alter application state.

If state must be shareable, URLs may exist.

But sitemap excludes them.

Internal SEO links target clean canonical routes.


## 10.27 — CANONICAL SIGNAL CONSISTENCY

Google uses multiple canonical signals, including:

- redirects;
- rel="canonical";
- sitemap inclusion;
- HTTPS preference and other indexing signals.

Therefore every signal must agree.

Never:

canonical → /mma/ sitemap → /club-mma-colomiers/ internal links → /mma-colomiers/

One owner.

One URL.


## 10.28 — METADATA REGISTRY

Metadata is structured data in code.

Concept:

```text
interface RouteMetadata {
route: string;
title: string;
description: string;
canonical: string;
robots: string;
ogTitle: string;
ogDescription: string;
ogImage: string;
ogImageAlt: string;
breadcrumbLabel: string;
primaryIntent: string;
schemaProfile: string;
}
```

Pages consume it.

No repeated hand-coded `<head>` logic.


## 10.29 — TITLE RULE

Every indexable page gets one concise, descriptive `<title>`.

Google currently recommends:

- unique titles;
- descriptive titles;
- avoiding vague labels;
- avoiding unnecessarily verbose/repetitive titles;
- making the main on-page title visually clear.

We already follow this.


## 10.30 — LOCKED TITLE SYSTEM

Recommended baseline:

HOME Boxe, MMA & sports de combat près de Colomiers | Boxing Center BOXE Boxe anglaise près de Colomiers | Boxing Center MMA MMA près de Colomiers | Où s'entraîner avec Boxing Center FITNESS Boxing fitness près de Colomiers | Boxing Center CHILDREN Boxe enfant près de Colomiers | Baby Boxe & éducative FIRST SESSION Débuter la boxe près de Colomiers | Première séance

CLUB ENGINE Quel Boxing Center choisir depuis Colomiers ? | Minimes ou Portet PLANNING Planning boxe & MMA depuis Colomiers | Boxing Center PRICING Tarifs boxe & MMA près de Colomiers | Boxing Center CONTACT Contact Boxing Center pour Colomiers

Final SERP-length tuning later.

No SEO stuffing.


## 10.31 — GOOGLE MAY REWRITE TITLES

Our `<title>` is a recommendation to Search, not a contractual display guarantee.

Google may use:

- <title>;
- page headings;
- prominent on-page copy;
- link text;
- other signals

to construct its displayed title.

Therefore:


### TITLE + H1 + VISUAL TITLE MUST AGREE SEMANTICALLY.

Not identical wording.

Same meaning.


## 10.32 — META DESCRIPTION

Every important page gets a unique description.

Purpose:

search-result persuasion/context.

Not direct ranking manipulation.

Google may use the provided description or may extract a different snippet from page content depending on the query.

Therefore:

meta description must be good.

Opening page content must also be excellent.


## 10.33 — DESCRIPTION FORMULA

USER SITUATION + WHAT PAGE RESOLVES + USEFUL DIFFERENTIATOR

Example:

Tu habites Colomiers et veux essayer le MMA ? Découvre où Boxing Center le propose réellement, les créneaux adaptés et ce qui t'attend lors d'une première séance.

No:

MMA Colomiers, club MMA Colomiers, sport combat Colomiers…


## 10.34 — SITE NAME

Google recommends `WebSite` structured data on the home page to indicate the preferred site name and says the name should be accurate, concise and consistent with the site's actual identity.

Our preferred site-name candidate:


### Boxing Center depuis Colomiers

Not:

**Boxing Center Colomiers**

because that formulation more strongly resembles a physical branch name.

Potential:

{ "@type": "WebSite", "name": "Boxing Center depuis Colomiers" }

No fake alternate name added merely for keywords.


## 10.35 — FAVICON

Use the official Boxing Center symbol/mark only if it remains readable and authorized.

Google currently recommends a square favicon with a stable URL and suggests a size larger than 48×48px for good rendering, while requiring crawlers to be able to access both homepage and favicon.

Provide:

favicon.svg where compatible favicon.ico fallback apple-touch-icon

No fake Colomiers location badge.


## 10.36 — OPEN GRAPH

Every canonical page receives:

<meta property="og:type" content="website"> <meta property="og:locale" content="fr_FR"> <meta property="og:site_name" content="Boxing Center depuis Colomiers"> <meta property="og:title" content="..."> <meta property="og:description" content="..."> <meta property="og:url" content="CANONICAL"> <meta property="og:image" content="ABSOLUTE IMAGE URL"> <meta property="og:image:alt" content="...">

Use absolute URLs.


## 10.37 — OG IMAGE SYSTEM

Preferred dimensions:

1200 × 630

Art-directed from Part 05.

Each OG image should expose:

- page intent;
- Boxing Center identity;
- DEPUIS COLOMIERS;
- Resolving Route visual;
- real physical club label where shown.

No page screenshots.

No AI-generated pseudo-gym imagery.


## 10.38 — SOCIAL CARD TRUTH

MMA card:

MMA DEPUIS COLOMIERS COLOMIERS

- ────────
- PORTET

Good.

Bad:

BOXING CENTER COLOMIERS MMA CLUB

The social preview is an entity surface too.

Truth rules still apply.


## 10.39 — TWITTER/X CARD

Use:

<meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image">

with same title/description/image architecture unless a platform-specific improvement is needed.

No need to maintain separate factual social copy.


## 10.40 — HTML LANGUAGE

At launch:

```text
<html lang="fr-FR">
```

or standards-valid appropriate language tagging.

The entire primary site is French.


## 10.41 — HREFLANG

At launch:


### NO HREFLANG COMPLEXITY

There is one language version.

Do not add fake English/Spanish alternates.

Do not add `x-default` merely because an SEO checklist says so.

If multilingual routes exist later:

implement reciprocal hreflang properly.

Until then:

simple French site.


## 10.42 — XML SITEMAP

The sitemap includes only URLs we genuinely want indexed.

Google recommends using the sitemap to list preferred canonical URLs.

Our indexable sitemap:

/

/boxe-anglaise/ /mma/ /boxing-fitness/ /boxe-enfants/ /premiere-seance/ /quel-club/ /plannings/ /tarifs/ /contact/

Legal route inclusion depends on their index policy.


## 10.43 — ASTRO SITEMAP

Use official:

@astrojs/sitemap

Astro's current sitemap integration requires the deployed `site` URL and can filter routes from generated sitemap output.

Config concept:

```text
export default defineConfig({
site: 'https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr',
integrations: [
sitemap({
filter: (page) => isPublicIndexableUrl(page)
})
]
});
```

One shared `isPublicIndexableUrl` policy should also drive QA.


## 10.44 — SITEMAP EXCLUSIONS

Exclude:

/__dev/* /api/* parameter states preview pages staging fixtures internal QA routes machine endpoints that are not search pages 404 redirect URLs noindex pages

Do not put everything Astro generated into the sitemap blindly.


## 10.45 — lastmod

Use `lastmod` only when content has materially changed.

Not:

build ran today → all pages updated today.

Bing specifically recommends accurate freshness signals in sitemaps, including meaningful `lastmod`.

For us:

`lastmod` derives from:

- content revision;
- claim changes;
- materially changed schedule/pricing content.

Not deploy timestamp.


## 10.46 — changefreq AND priority

Astro's current sitemap documentation notes that Google ignores sitemap `changefreq` and

`priority`.

Therefore:

we do not spend effort inventing:

<priority>1.0</priority> <changefreq>daily</changefreq>

for SEO theatre.

Use meaningful `lastmod`.

That matters.


## 10.47 — ROBOTS.TXT ROLE

Robots controls **crawl access**.

It does not reliably function as a search-index removal mechanism.

Google notes that `noindex` directives can only be read when crawlers are allowed to fetch the page.

Bing explicitly states:

robots.txt controls crawl access, not indexing; use NOINDEX when a URL should not appear in Bing/Copilot.

Therefore:


### DO NOT USE ROBOTS AS SECURITY.


## 10.48 — PUBLIC ROBOTS BASELINE

Conceptual:

User-agent: * Allow: / Disallow: /__dev/ Disallow: /api/internal/ Sitemap: https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/sitemap-index.xml

Exact private paths depend on implementation.


## 10.49 — PRIVATE ROUTES

Best solution:


### DO NOT DEPLOY THEM PUBLICLY

or:

authenticate them.

Robots is not access control.

A secret route disallowed in robots is still public if someone knows the URL.


## 10.50 — STAGING

Staging must be protected with:

- authentication/access restriction;
- noindex;

- ideally X-Robots-Tag: noindex, nofollow;
- no public internal links;
- no sitemap exposure.

Do not rely only on:

```text
Disallow: /
```

Staging leaks are one of the easiest ways to create duplicate/indexing mess.


## 10.51 — PRODUCTION NOINDEX POLICY

Expected indexable:

/ /boxe-anglaise/ /mma/ /boxing-fitness/ /boxe-enfants/ /premiere-seance/ /quel-club/ /plannings/ /tarifs/ /contact/

Recommended possible `noindex`:

privacy/legal pages if we decide they have no organic discovery role internal confirmation pages form success states temporary previews

No canonical SEO route accidentally receives `noindex`.


## 10.52 — QUERY STATES AND NOINDEX

Do not create:

```text
/mma/?club=portet
```

as independently indexed pages.

Prefer:

clean canonical.

But do **not** plaster `noindex` onto every parameter state when canonicalization and non-crawlable filter implementation already control duplication.

One clean URL architecture is better than conflicting directives.


## 10.53 — OAI-SEARCHBOT

OpenAI's current publisher guidance says public sites that want their content eligible for ChatGPT Search summaries/snippets should not block:


### OAI-SearchBot

Therefore:

for our GEO objective:


### OAI-SEARCHBOT MUST BE ALLOWED TO ACCESS PUBLIC INDEXABLE CONTENT.

Also check:

- CDN;
- Cloudflare/bot protections;
- WAF;
- rate limits.

Robots alone is not enough if infrastructure returns 403.

OpenAI explicitly warns that security layers can accidentally block its crawlers and publishes crawler IP information for verification.


## 10.54 — GPTBOT IS A SEPARATE POLICY

OpenAI treats:

- OAI-SearchBot — search/discovery;

- GPTBot — potential foundation-model training;

as separate controls.

Therefore the business can choose:


### Search visibility + training allowed

Allow both.


### Search visibility + training excluded

Allow:

OAI-SearchBot

and disallow:

GPTBot

This does not require sacrificing ChatGPT Search visibility.


## 10.55 — GPTBOT DECISION IS NOT AN SEO DECISION

Do not let the implementation LLM decide this.

It is:


### A BUSINESS/DATA POLICY DECISION

The blueprint should expose a configuration variable:

```text
allowFoundationModelTraining: true | false
```

Then robots generator applies the chosen policy.

SearchBot remains allowed either way.


## 10.56 — CHATGPT REFERRAL TRACKING

OpenAI's current publisher guidance says ChatGPT search referral URLs use:

utm_source=chatgpt.com

which publishers can use for traffic analysis.

Therefore analytics reporting should include:


### CHATGPT REFERRAL TRAFFIC

as a dedicated acquisition source.


## 10.57 — LLMS.TXT

We may generate:

/llms.txt /llms-full.txt

as supplemental machine-readable summaries.

But critical distinction:


### THEY ARE NOT THE CANONICAL CONTENT.

Google currently says there are no additional AI-specific requirements for its generative Search features.

OpenAI's current search-publisher guidance focuses on crawl access via `OAI-SearchBot`, not

on requiring `llms.txt`.

Therefore:

`llms.txt` is an **ecosystem convenience**, not our GEO strategy.


## 10.58 — LLMS.TXT CONTENT

Generated automatically.

Potential:

# Boxing Center — Depuis Colomiers Purpose: This website helps people living in Colomiers identify relevant Boxing Center clubs. Important: There is no physical Boxing Center gym located in Colomiers. Physical clubs presented: - Boxing Center Toulouse Minimes - Boxing Center Portet-sur-Garonne Main routes: - English boxing - MMA - Boxing fitness - Children's boxing - First session

- Club comparison - Schedules - Pricing - Contact Canonical website: https://www.boxingcenter-colomiers.fr/

Then canonical links.

No marketing prose.


## 10.59 — LLMS-FULL

Could provide deeper verified:

- entity definitions;
- discipline availability;
- contact;
- page inventory;
- current offers;
- schedule summary.

But volatile facts are generated from registry.

Never manually maintained.


## 10.60 — MACHINE FILE FRESHNESS

Machine files rebuild whenever their underlying claims change.

Not independently.

If Portet MMA disappears:

same commit/build updates:

HTML

JSON-LD llms comparison recommendation engine

One source.


## 10.61 — NO ai.txt

No arbitrary extra AI-control file unless a recognized ecosystem use emerges.

No inventing protocol-looking files to appear technically advanced.


## 10.62 — NO PUBLIC MCP AT LAUNCH

Do **not** expose:

```text
/api/mcp
```

merely because previous sites experimented with agent interfaces.

A public MCP endpoint must have an actual product use.

This site's core content is already crawlable and structured.

At launch:

not needed.

Avoid unnecessary attack surface and complexity.


## 10.63 — GOOGLE AI SEARCH

Google's current guidance emphasizes:

- unique/non-commodity content;
- useful local content;
- clear site structure;
- accessible crawlable pages;
- standard SEO fundamentals.

That validates our core strategy:


### THE PROXIMITY ENGINE + TRUTHFUL LOCAL ORIENTATION IS OUR GEO ADVANTAGE.

Not AI meta tags.


## 10.64 — GOOGLE SEARCH CONSOLE AI REPORT

As of **August 31, 2026**, Google says its dedicated generative-AI performance insights in Search Console have rolled out worldwide.

Therefore post-launch reporting should explicitly monitor:

traditional Search visibility + generative Search visibility

We no longer need to guess AI visibility solely from referral logs.


## 10.65 — BING / COPILOT

Current Bing Webmaster Guidelines explicitly cover:

- Bing Search;
- Copilot;
- grounding/citation experiences;

and say discoverable, accurate, well-structured content supports all of them.

Therefore:

Bing Webmaster Tools becomes a first-class launch tool.

Not optional afterthought.


## 10.66 — INDEXNOW

Bing currently strongly recommends IndexNow for faster automated notification of:

- added URLs;
- updated URLs;
- deleted URLs.

For this small Astro site:

implementation is straightforward.


## 10.67 — INDEXNOW EVENTS

Submit when:

new canonical page published meaningful content update price update schedule page materially updates URL redirected page removed

Do not submit every deployment if page content did not change.


## 10.68 — INDEXNOW DOES NOT GUARANTEE INDEXING

IndexNow tells participating engines something changed.

It does not force inclusion.

Bing explicitly says submission does not guarantee indexing.

No:

“instant SEO indexing guaranteed.”


## 10.69 — INDEXNOW KEY

Host the validation key according to the official protocol.

Potential:

/{key}.txt

Do not accidentally hide it behind application routing/auth.


## 10.70 — GOOGLE DOES NOT USE INDEXNOW AS OUR GOOGLE SUBMISSION STRATEGY

IndexNow supports participating search engines such as Bing and others.

For Google:

use:

- sitemap;
- crawlable links;
- Search Console URL Inspection/request recrawl when appropriate.

Different ecosystem.


## 10.71 — INTERNAL LINK ARCHITECTURE

Every important page is reachable through normal crawlable `<a href>` links.

No route exists only because JavaScript knows its path.

This was already locked in Parts 02–08.

Machine architecture now enforces it.


## 10.72 — LINK GRAPH

Conceptual:

```text
HOME
│
├── BOXE
│ ├── FIRST SESSION
│ ├── CLUB ENGINE
│ └── PLANNING
│
├── MMA
│ ├── FIRST SESSION
│ ├── CLUB ENGINE
│ └── PLANNING
│
├── FITNESS
│ ├── CLUB ENGINE
│ └── PLANNING
│
├── CHILDREN
│ ├── PLANNING
│ └── PRICING
│
├── CLUB ENGINE
├── PLANNING
├── PRICING
└── CONTACT
```

No orphan pages.


## 10.73 — ANCHOR TEXT

Descriptive.

Google recommends meaningful crawlable link text. This also improves human information scent.

Good:

Voir les créneaux MMA

Comparer Minimes et Portet

Découvrir Boxing Center Portet

Bad:

Cliquez ici


## 10.74 — EXTERNAL BACKLINKS FROM COLOMIERS

The formal brief requires:

- direct Minimes links from planning/tariffs;
- Portet backlink in homepage content.

We implement those naturally.

No excessive sitewide exact-match anchor repetition.


## 10.75 — INBOUND NETWORK LINKS

Strategically, the existing Boxing Center ecosystem should also link **to Colomiers where** **contextually relevant**, for example from:

- network location resources;
- nearby-area guidance;
- blog/local coverage if available.

This establishes the site as a legitimate network resource rather than an isolated SEO satellite.

But only create such links where they help users.

No artificial link wheel.


## 10.76 — NETWORK LINK REGISTRY

Central data:

networkSites = { minimes: "...", portet: "...", colomiers: "...", ... }

All sister links use this registry.

No hard-coded domain strings inside page copy/components.

This directly eliminates the stale Vercel-link defect found in our previous sites.


## 10.77 — REDIRECT REGISTRY

Maintain explicit:

redirects = { "/club-boxe-colomiers/": "/", "/club-mma-colomiers/": "/mma/", ... }

Only if those old/alternate URLs ever existed or received links.

Do not create redirects for hundreds of hypothetical keyword URLs.


## 10.78 — HTTP STATUS POLICY


### 200

Canonical content.


### 301/308

Permanent migration.


### 404

Unknown/missing URL.


### 410

Content intentionally and permanently removed without replacement where appropriate.


### 503

Temporary service/maintenance outage.

Never return:

200

for a fake 404.


## 10.79 — SOFT 404 BAN

If page doesn't exist:

return actual 404.

If a discipline is discontinued but there is a clear replacement:

301 to the most relevant replacement.

Do not redirect every dead URL to homepage.

That confuses both humans and crawlers.


## 10.80 — IMAGE SEO

Google currently recommends:

- descriptive filenames;
- relevant nearby page text;
- useful alt text;
- consistent image URLs;
- crawlable image resources.

This matches Part 09.


## 10.81 — IMAGE FILENAME TRUTH

Because supplied imagery is from real gyms such as Minimes:

Good:

boxing-center-minimes-ring.webp

Bad:

club-boxe-colomiers-salle.webp

if photograph is actually Toulouse Minimes.

The page provides Colomiers relevance.

The image filename describes the image.


## 10.82 — IMAGE URL STABILITY

Do not fingerprint image URLs in a way that unnecessarily produces a completely new public path for every tiny rebuild if SEO image continuity matters.

Build optimization must balance caching/versioning and URL stability.

Important editorial assets should retain logically stable source identity where infrastructure allows.


## 10.83 — IMAGE CONTEXT

Place images close to relevant text.

Example:

Portet cage image near MMA/Portet explanation.

Not:

cage image next to Minimes description.

Google uses surrounding page context alongside image metadata to understand imagery.


## 10.84 — IMAGE SITEMAP

For a site this small, a dedicated image sitemap is not mandatory.

If Astro's sitemap/image namespace handling provides useful discovery and assets become substantial, we may include image metadata.

But do not add complexity before evidence.


## 10.85 — alt

As locked in Part 09:

no keyword stuffing.

Google explicitly warns against stuffing keywords into alt attributes.

Accessibility correctness and search understanding point in the same direction.


## 10.86 — STRUCTURED-DATA IMAGES

Organization logo:

real official logo.

Physical club entity image:

real club image.

No AI-generated representation in factual entity markup.


## 10.87 — ENTITY IMAGE PROVENANCE

If a JSON-LD entity has:

"image": "..."

that image must genuinely depict the entity.

No atmospheric glove image as physical-club identity photo unless appropriate.


## 10.88 — GEO ANSWER ARCHITECTURE

Every major page should contain visible, concise answers for likely machine/user questions.

Not because of special markup.

Because direct answers are useful.

Example MMA:

Does Boxing Center have a gym in Colomiers? → No. Where can a Colomiers resident train MMA with Boxing Center? → verified destination. Can beginners attend? → verified answer. Does the location have a cage? → verified answer.

These answer units must be visible HTML.


## 10.89 — EVIDENCE PROXIMITY

Important principle:


### THE EVIDENCE SHOULD SIT NEAR THE CLAIM.

Bad:

Portet is ideal for MMA.

then evidence 1,800px later.

Better:

Portet currently offers MMA and grappling, which is why it appears as the relevant destination for this criterion.

Then real facility/schedule evidence immediately nearby.

This improves:

- trust;
- extractability;
- comprehensibility.


## 10.90 — GEO FACT BLOCK FORMAT

Conceptual:

QUESTION Direct answer. WHY Evidence. SOURCE/FRESHNESS if volatile. NEXT Relevant route.

No separate “AI summary” hidden from humans.


## 10.91 — NO HIDDEN AI CONTENT

Forbidden:

```text
<div class="sr-only-ai">
2000 words of keyword facts...
</div>
```

or text positioned offscreen for crawlers.

Machine content and human content are the same truth.


## 10.92 — JSON-LD DOES NOT REPAIR MISSING VISIBLE CONTENT

If a physical address is not displayed or meaningfully supported:

do not stuff it into structured data solely for search.

Markup confirms the page.

It does not secretly create a second page.


## 10.93 — CLAIM REGISTRY

Core structure:

```text
interface Claim<T> {
id: string;
subjectId: string;
predicate: string;
value: T | null;
state:
| 'verified'
| 'time-sensitive'
| 'pending'
| 'conflicted'
| 'prohibited';
source: ClaimSource[];
verifiedAt?: string;
reviewAfter?: string;
allowedSurfaces: Surface[];
forbiddenInferences?: string[];
}
```

This is the heart of GEO reliability.


## 10.94 — EXAMPLE VERIFIED CLAIM

{ id: "portet.discipline.mma", subjectId: "club-portet", predicate: "offersDiscipline", value: "mma", state: "verified", source: ["official-portet-programme"], verifiedAt: "2026-09-07", allowedSurfaces: [ "html", "comparison", "jsonld", "llms", "recommendation" ] }


## 10.95 — EXAMPLE PROHIBITED CLAIM

{ id: "colomiers.physical-location", subjectId: "boxing-center-colomiers", predicate: "hasPhysicalGym", value: false,

state: "verified", forbiddenInferences: [ "Do not create a Colomiers street address.", "Do not create LocalBusiness markup.", "Do not refer to 'notre salle à Colomiers'.", "Do not generate a map pin in Colomiers." ] }

This is extremely powerful for implementation safety.


## 10.96 — CONFLICTED CLAIM EXAMPLE

Current brief:

10h–21h15.

Current public location evidence we reviewed earlier:

21h30.

Registry:

{ id: "network.opening-hours", state: "conflicted", sources: [ "client-brief", "current-club-site" ] }

Build behavior:


### DO NOT PUBLISH A RESOLVED VALUE UNTIL VERIFIED.


## 10.97 — CLAIM SOURCE TYPES

Potential:

CLIENT BRIEF OFFICIAL CLUB SITE OFFICIAL BUSINESS DATA DIRECT CLUB CONFIRMATION OFFICIAL FEDERATION OFFICIAL PRODUCT DATA VERIFIED INTERNAL DATA

Search-engine snippets are not authoritative sources.

Neither is an AI response.


## 10.98 — FRESHNESS CLASSES


### STATIC

Entity name.


### LOW VOLATILITY

Address.


### MEDIUM

Facilities/coaches.


### HIGH

Schedules.


### VERY HIGH

Promotional prices/offers.

Each class receives an appropriate review cadence.


## 10.99 — EXPIRATION

Offers with known end date:

validFrom validThrough

internally where appropriate.

At expiry:

automatic build/test prevents public rendering.

No stale promotion should survive because someone forgot to remove copy.


## 10.100 — STRUCTURED DATA GENERATION

Never maintain JSON-LD separately by page.

Functions:

buildWebsiteSchema() buildOrganizationSchema() buildWebPageSchema(route) buildClubEntity(clubId) buildBreadcrumbSchema(route)

All ingest the registry.


## 10.101 — STRUCTURED DATA TESTS

Automated tests:

No LocalBusiness named Colomiers No Colomiers PostalAddress on Boxing Center entity All @ids stable All URLs canonical HTTPS All physical addresses verified JSON-LD prices == visible prices JSON-LD opening hours == visible opening hours No expired offers No aggregateRating attributed to Colomiers No undefined fields No staging URLs

These are release blockers.


## 10.102 — RICH RESULTS TEST

Google recommends validating supported structured data using its Rich Results Test and then inspecting deployed URLs through Search Console.

QA workflow:

build ↓ schema validation ↓ Rich Results Test for eligible features ↓ production deploy ↓ URL Inspection


## 10.103 — SCHEMA.ORG VALIDATION

Because not all useful Schema.org markup corresponds to a Google rich-result feature:

also validate graph syntax/semantics independently.

Rich-results eligibility and semantic correctness are not identical concepts.


## 10.104 — NO MARKUP ERRORS IN PRODUCTION

Warnings may be evaluated case-by-case.

Errors:

release blocker when they affect intended structured-data functionality.

No:

“Google will figure it out.”


## 10.105 — CANONICAL BUILD TEST

For each page:

HTTP URL → canonical HTTPS non-www → www wrong slash → canonical slash canonical tag → exact final URL sitemap → exact final URL OG URL

→ exact final URL JSON-LD URL → exact final URL

One string identity.


## 10.106 — STAGING URL BAN

Production output must contain **zero**:

localhost 127.0.0.1 .vercel.app .netlify.app pages.dev preview.* staging.*

unless a legitimate external asset service is explicitly approved.

Automated grep test.


## 10.107 — URL STRUCTURE

Routes remain human:

/mma/ /boxe-enfants/ /premiere-seance/

No:

```text
/page?id=52
```

No:

```text
/seo-club-boxe-colomiers-pas-cher/
```

URLs reflect information architecture.


## 10.108 — SLUG STABILITY

Once indexed and receiving links:

do not rename casually for SEO experimentation.

A slightly imperfect stable URL is often better than endless slug churn.

If changed:

permanent redirect + updated links + sitemap + IndexNow notification.


## 10.109 — TITLE/URL CANNIBALIZATION CHECK

Before launch:

query matrix confirms exactly one owner.

If:

HOME title = Club de boxe Colomiers BOXE title = Club de boxe Colomiers

fail.

Each route expresses distinct intent.


## 10.110 — LOCAL SEO MODEL

Because actual local businesses exist in Colomiers and Boxing Center does not:

we compete as an **informational/local-service alternative**, not as a fake local listing.

Therefore:

No Colomiers GBP creation for a non-existent location.

No fake citations.

No fake local NAP.

No fake branch directories.

No virtual-office address.


## 10.111 — NAP CONSISTENCY

For actual Minimes/Portet entities:

where displayed:

NAME ADDRESS PHONE

must match the authoritative current location data.

No partial variants that create entity confusion.


## 10.112 — COLOMIERS NAP

There is none.

The Colomiers site has:

- website;
- network contact point;
- orientation context.

Not a physical NAP entity.

This distinction is permanent unless an actual Colomiers location later opens.


## 10.113 — FUTURE COLOMIERS CLUB

If a real Boxing Center opens in Colomiers later:

this entire assumption changes.

Then:

1. claim registry changes; 2. entity graph changes; 3. site positioning changes; 4. LocalBusiness/SportsClub becomes legitimate; 5. maps/contact/address update; 6. content rewritten; 7. search intent may become transactional physical-local.

Do not simply add an address to the existing architecture.

That would be a strategic migration.


## 10.114 — GEO PROVENANCE LAYER

Machines should be able to encounter:

FACT + ENTITY + SOURCE RELATIONSHIP + FRESHNESS

where humanly useful.

Not necessarily formal citations everywhere.

Examples:

Programme actuel · Boxing Center Portet

Planning mis à jour · 7 septembre 2026

Image · Boxing Center Minimes, Toulouse

This increases machine and human clarity simultaneously.


## 10.115 — AUTHORSHIP / PUBLISHER

The site publisher/organization should be explicitly identifiable.

Potential:

"publisher": { "@id": ".../#organization" }

No invented individual author.
